# Kollink 管理後台 Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** 管理員在 `/admin` 審核創作者（核准、退回附原因、停權／解除）、看全部合作案並在爭議時平台取消（已付款自動標記需退款）、人工標記已付款（線下 MAN 單與綠界通知遺失的 KL 單，一律經由 `record_payment_success`）、看完整收款帳戶後標記撥款（拒絕測試付款）、處理需退款與付款異常、檢視違規訊息；創作者在「我的 → 收款帳戶」填銀行帳戶、被退回時看到原因並重新送審。README 的人工 SQL 改成以 service_role 呼叫同一批函式的備用 SQL。

**Architecture:** 兩個 migration：`20261128000100_admin_core.sql`（`is_admin()`／`my_role()` 收回 anon、anon 的兩條 policy 拆開、`admin_actor()`、`audit_logs.subject_id`、`creator_profiles` 的 `rejected` 與 `review_note`、`set_creator_status`、`request_creator_review`、`message_reviews`、列表函式）與 `20261128000200_admin_money.sql`（`creator_payout_accounts`、`payments.admin_note`、`collaboration_payouts`、`transition_collaboration` 三處修改、人工付款兩條路、撥款、看帳戶、平台取消、列表與總覽）。每個後台寫入都是 definer 函式（`admin_actor()` 判斷 admin／system、寫 audit）。TS 端：`src/lib/admin/`（網址參數、說明驗證、Row 轉換、錯誤文字、伺服器讀取）、`src/lib/payout-account.ts`、`requireAdmin()`、`src/app/admin/actions.ts`、`/admin` 五個頁面（與品牌區相同的殼）、創作者的收款帳戶頁與審核橫幅。

**Tech Stack:** Next.js 16.4.6（App Router、`cacheComponents`、Server Actions、`proxy.ts`）、React 19.3、TypeScript、Tailwind v4、Supabase CLI 3.2（Postgres 17）、`@supabase/supabase-js` 2.119、pgTAP、Vitest（node 環境）、lucide-react。

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-27-admin-design.md`（含使用者決定與代替使用者做的決定；延後事項與既有決定見 `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-22-foundation-followups.md`）

## Global Constraints

- Next.js 16 與訓練資料不同：寫程式前先讀 `node_modules/next/dist/docs/` 對應文件（本計畫用到的已列在下方「設計決定」）。`middleware.ts` 已改名為 `proxy.ts`。
- `cacheComponents` 已開啟：讀 `cookies()`、`searchParams`、`params` 的元件必須在 `<Suspense>` 內；不要在 layout 最外層 await session。不可使用 `export const runtime`、`export const dynamic`。讀現在時間（`nowMs()`）只能在 `requireAdmin()`／`requireRole()` 之後（內部經由 `getCurrentUser` 已 `await connection()`）。
- 守門：每個 `/admin` 頁面的 Suspense 內容與每個管理員 Server Action 開頭都呼叫 `requireAdmin()`（layout 的 Gate 不算數，用戶端換頁時不會重跑）。創作者的頁面與 action 照舊 `requireRole("creator")`。
- 權限只在資料庫：每個後台寫入都經由 definer 函式（`admin_actor()` 檢查 `is_admin()`，或 service_role 的備用 SQL），寫 `audit_logs`（`actor_kind` = `admin`／`system`）。Server Action 一律用**管理員自己的** client（`src/lib/supabase/server.ts`），**不可**用 `createAdminClient()`（service_role 會被判定為 system，而且繞過 RLS）。唯一例外是既有的本機測試快速登入 route（只在 `isDevLoginEnabled()`）。
- 管理員是合作案的參與者時，只能以參與者身分操作（`transition_collaboration` 的身分優先順序；新函式回 `admin is participant`），畫面不顯示管理員動作。
- 收款帳號：`creator_payout_accounts.account_number` 不授權給任何 API 角色直接讀；完整帳號只能經由 `admin_get_payout_account`（只在 `awaiting_payout`、會寫 audit），只出現在 `/admin/collabs/[id]` 的撥款區。其他地方（列表、創作者本人、撥款紀錄）一律只用 `account_last4`。品牌永遠拿不到任何帳戶欄位。不要把完整帳號寫進 log、audit 或錯誤訊息。
- 參與者看得到合作案的 `audit_logs`（事件列表）：管理員的內部說明不放 audit 的 note（人工付款說明 → `payments.admin_note`；轉帳說明 → `collaboration_payouts.transfer_note`）。平台取消的原因是例外（雙方都要知道）。
- SQL 函式一律 `set search_path = ''`，物件名稱寫完整 schema；`RETURNS TABLE` 的 plpgsql 函式加 `#variable_conflict use_column`，欄位一律加表別名。新資料表先 `revoke all ... from anon, authenticated, service_role` 再明確 `grant`；新函式先 `revoke execute ... from public, anon, authenticated, service_role` 再 `grant` 給需要的角色；內部 helper 不 grant 給任何 API 角色（followups「資料表權限寫在 migration」）。
- 錯誤碼（TS 端只透過 `src/lib/admin/errors.ts` 轉成中文）：身分 `42501`（`not admin`、`admin is participant`、`not a creator`）；找不到 `P0002`（`creator not found`、`collaboration not found`、`payment not found`、`message not found`）；狀態 `55000`（`creator status changed`、`collaboration not awaiting payment`、`collaboration not awaiting payout`、`collaboration already closed`、`test payment cannot be paid out`、`no paid payment`、`refund pending`、`payout account missing`）；格式 `22023`（`invalid action`、`invalid status`、`invalid view`、`invalid reason`、`invalid note`、`invalid env`、`invalid provider_trade_no`、`invalid amount`、`not an online payment`、`message not flagged`、`invalid bank_code`、`invalid branch_code`、`invalid account_number`、`invalid account_name`）。
- 數字常數兩邊要一致：說明上限 500（TS `ADMIN_NOTE_MAX` ↔ SQL `admin_clean_note`）；收款帳戶規則（TS `src/lib/payout-account.ts` ↔ SQL `set_my_payout_account`）；每頁 50、上限 100（TS `ADMIN_PAGE_SIZE` ↔ SQL `least(greatest(p_limit, 1), 100)`）；關鍵字 50 字（TS `ADMIN_QUERY_MAX` ↔ SQL `left(…, 50)`）。兩邊的註解互相指向。
- 網址參數（`?status=`、`?view=`、`?state=`、`?q=`、`?page=`）一律容忍不認得的值（退回預設，不報錯）；Server Action 的參數一律當 `unknown` 檢查（`isUuid` 等）。`"use server"` 檔案只能 export async 函式（型別可以）。
- `supabase gen types` 產生的型別把 `RETURNS TABLE` 欄位都標成非 null：一律自己定義欄位可為 null 的 Row 型別，`data` 先轉 `unknown` 再轉型（`src/lib/admin/view.ts`）。
- 確認一律用頁面內的區塊：沒有輸入的用既有 `ConfirmAction`，要寫原因／說明的用新的 `NoteAction`；不可用 `confirm()`。送出按鈕進行中一律 `disabled`。
- 介面文字為繁體中文；產品名稱只從 `src/lib/site.ts` 的 `SITE_NAME` 取得。顏色只用 `src/app/globals.css` 的 token class（`bg-surface`、`bg-primary-soft`、`border-line`、`border-danger`、`text-ink`、`text-muted`、`text-danger`、`text-success`、`bg-success-soft`、`bg-ink`、`text-on-ink`…）；圓角 `rounded-card`、`rounded-btn`、`rounded-input`。不可用 `text-white` 或色碼。手機 390 寬不可出現整頁水平捲軸（長字串 `break-all`／`break-words`、`min-w-0`）；可點的目標高度至少 40px（篩選標籤 44px）。
- Server 元件不可從 `"use client"` 檔案 import 非元件的值（例如 `components/forms/Field.tsx` 的 `inputClass`）：後台共用的 class 放 `src/components/admin/styles.ts`。
- 不新增 npm 套件。E2E 用的 playwright-core 裝在 scratchpad，不加進專案。
- 只動**本機** Supabase：不可執行 `supabase link`、`db push --remote`、`supabase config push`，也不可連到正式專案或 Vercel 正式環境變數。
- 不可同時跑兩個 `next build`／`next dev`／`next start`（共用 `.next`）；只有 Task 10 會啟動伺服器（一個 dev server、port 3000），用完要關掉。
- 每個 task 結束時：`npm run lint && npm run typecheck && npm test` 全過；有動到 SQL 的 task 另跑 `npx supabase test db` 全過。畫面元件沒有單元測試環境（vitest 是 node 環境），由 typecheck、lint、Task 10 的 build 與 E2E 驗證。
- pgTAP fixture 一律寫在該測試檔自己的 transaction 裡（結尾 `rollback`），id 用本計畫專屬的前綴（`…000a0x`、`…000a1x`、`…000a2x`、`…000ac*`、`…0000aa*`／`ab*`／`ad*`、`a9c8a9c8-…`），handle 用 `admtest_*`、`admmoney_*`；列表斷言一律用這些前綴或關鍵字過濾（本機資料庫可能有 E2E 留下的資料）。
- Commit 訊息用中文寫「計畫 9 Task N：重點」，結尾加你所在環境指定的 Co-Authored-By 歸屬行（寫實際執行的模型）。
- 前置：Docker Desktop 已啟動，`npx supabase start` 已執行，`.env.local` 已設定（見 README「本機開發」）。每個 DB task 開始前先 `npx supabase migration up` 套用新 migration。

## 設計決定

讀過的文件：
- `node_modules/next/dist/docs/01-app/02-guides/authentication-with-cache-components.md`（session 讀取放在 `<Suspense>` 內、不在 layout 最外層 await）
- `node_modules/next/dist/docs/01-app/02-guides/server-actions.md`（action 是公開的 POST；每個 action 自己驗證身分）
- `node_modules/next/dist/docs/01-app/03-api-reference/03-file-conventions/proxy.md`（`proxy.ts` 只做樂觀檢查）
- 既有程式：`src/lib/auth/current-user.ts`（`requireRole`）、`src/app/auth/dev-login/route.ts`、`src/components/shell/*`、`src/components/briefs/ConfirmAction.tsx`、`src/lib/collabs/load.ts`、`src/lib/payments/view.ts`、`supabase/migrations/20261125000500_collab_text_guards.sql`（目前的 `transition_collaboration`）、`20261127000100_payment_orders.sql`、`20261127000200_payment_review_fixes.sql`

決定：
1. **兩個 migration、兩個新 pgTAP 檔**（`24_admin_core.test.sql` 48 個、`25_admin_money.test.sql` 60 個），另改 `03_collaborations.test.sql` 一處（直接 `payout` 前先設 `kollink.paying_out` 旗標，總數 44 不變）。本計畫撰寫時已把兩個 migration、新測試、改過的 `03`，以及其他 22 個既有測試檔，在本機 rollback 的 transaction 裡實際跑過，全部通過（`03` 44、`05` 122、`08` 83、`18` 51、`22` 54、`24` 48、`25` 60……）；README 的備用 SQL 也以 service_role 跑過（線下付款、通知遺失、看帳戶、撥款、平台取消、停權；案號比對到多個合作案時整段報錯）。TS 程式（Task 3–8）在 scratchpad 的副本跑過 `tsc --noEmit`、`eslint`、`vitest`（74 個檔、691 個測試全過；型別是依 migration 手寫的近似值，實作時以 `npm run db:types` 產生的為準）。`next build` 沒跑過（Task 10 會跑）。
2. **`is_admin()`／`my_role()`**：撰寫時查過本機資料庫的 `pg_policies`（含 storage）、view、invoker 函式：會以 anon（或 PUBLIC）套用、又呼叫這兩個函式的 policy 只有 `creator_profiles_select` 與 `creator_portfolio_items_select`，拆成 anon 專用（只看 approved、不呼叫函式）與 authenticated 兩條；其他呼叫者都是 authenticated 的 policy 或 definer 函式。收回 PUBLIC／anon，grant 給 authenticated 與 service_role。pgTAP 用 `pg_policies` 斷言不會再出現 anon／PUBLIC 的 policy 呼叫它們。
3. **管理員身分**：`admins` 表為準，與 `profiles.role` 無關；`requireAdmin()` 用 `rpc("is_admin")`（`getIsAdmin`，React `cache`）。沒有 profile 的管理員登入後到 `/admin`（`resolvePostLoginPath` 多一個 `isAdmin`；`/onboarding` 的 Gate 也導去 `/admin`）。`proxy.ts` 不改程式，只在 `routes.ts` 的需要登入路徑加 `/admin`。
4. **`admin_actor()`**：管理員 → `admin`；service_role（README 備用 SQL）→ `system`（與 `resolve_payment_refund` 相同寫法）；其他 → `42501 not admin`。所有後台函式開頭呼叫，列表函式也是（`perform`）。
5. **審核**：`creator_profiles.status` 加 `rejected`，`review_note`（CHECK：只有 rejected／suspended 可以有值）。handle 鎖定 trigger 改成只有 approved／suspended 才鎖（退回不鎖，創作者修正時還能改網址代稱）。`request_creator_review()` 給被退回的創作者。創作者在自己的 `creator_profiles` 列讀 `review_note`（`getCurrentUser` 多選一欄）；公開讀得到的只有 approved 列，而 approved 一定沒有原因。
6. **audit 的 `subject_id`**：不新增「管理員操作紀錄」表；`collaboration_id` 為 null 的 audit 列本來就只有管理員讀得到（`audit_logs_select` 的 exists 不成立）。`list_collaboration_events` 不用改。
7. **`transition_collaboration`** 以 `create or replace` 整段重寫（與 `20261125000500` 相同，只改三處，見 Task 2）：`mark_paid` 允許 admin（仍要 `kollink.recording_payment`）；`payout` 要 `kollink.paying_out`；新增 `admin_cancel`（要 `kollink.admin_cancelling` 與原因）。旗標只在 definer 函式裡 `set_config(…, true)`，PostgREST 的使用者無法設定。
8. **人工付款**：`admin_record_offline_payment` 在函式裡建 MAN 單（`MAN` + 17 位大寫十六進位 = 20 字），`admin_record_lost_payment` 用既有 KL 單並先寫 `admin_note`。兩者都回傳 `record_payment_success` 的結果；Server Action 用 `paymentResultNotice` 把 `anomaly` 當失敗、`paid_refund_required`／`already_paid` 當成功但附說明。`p_env` 由 App 依 `readEcpayConfig()?.env` 帶入。
9. **撥款**：`admin_mark_payout` 的檢查順序與 `payoutBlocker()`（TS）相同：測試付款 → 沒有已付款 → 待退款 → 沒有收款帳戶。撥款金額 = 報價。`collaboration_payouts` 是帳戶快照（只留末四碼），帳戶之後被改也不影響紀錄。
10. **看完整帳號的 audit**：寫在 `admin_get_payout_account`（頁面渲染時呼叫的 volatile 函式，`supabase-js` 的 rpc 用 POST，可以寫入），10 分鐘內去重。帳戶 7 天內改過時撥款區顯示紅字（`accountChangedRecently`）。
11. **違規訊息**：`message_reviews` 獨立一張表（不改 `messages`：`messages` 在 Realtime publication，改欄位會對參與者送 UPDATE 事件，也會讓參與者讀到檢視狀態）。加部分索引 `messages_flagged_idx`。
12. **列表**：全部是 definer 函式（`admin_list_*`，window 函式算 `total_count`）；只有付款異常直接讀 `payment_anomalies`（本來就只有管理員讀得到，`count: "exact"`）。`admin_list_payments` 的 `p_collab_id` 放最後並給預設 null（PostgREST 才能省略）。
13. **畫面**：`/admin/collabs/[id]` 重用 `loadCollabDetail`（`get_collaboration` 對管理員回傳 `my_side = 'admin'`；管理員也是參與者時 `side` 是 brand／creator，畫面改顯示「請另一位管理員處理」）。付款單另外用 `admin_list_payments`（要看單號、交易編號、說明）。`EventList` 的 `chatHref` 傳 null。
14. **事件文字**：`admin_cancel` →「平台取消合作：原因」；`refund_scheduled` →「平台會退回已託管的款項」。已取消的卡片說明從「品牌已取消這個合作案。」改成「這個合作案已取消，原因見下方的紀錄。」（也可能是平台取消）。
15. **錯誤的「狀態已變」**：新增 `ADMIN_STALE_MESSAGE`（「資料已經更新」），加進 `isStaleStateMessage`，`ConfirmAction`／`NoteAction` 遇到時重新整理而不是顯示錯誤。
16. **測試快速登入**：`DEV_ACCOUNTS.admin`、`isDevAccount()`；route 對 admin 只建 auth 使用者並 upsert `admins`（不建 profile），導到 `/admin`。順手修 followups 記的「沒有 body 的 POST 會當掉」（`formData().catch`）。
17. **Soft delete 不做**、**通知不做**、**管理員管理畫面不做**（spec「不在範圍內」）。

## File Structure

| 檔案 | 責任 |
|---|---|
| `supabase/migrations/20261128000100_admin_core.sql` | 函式權限、anon policy 拆分、`admin_actor`、`admin_clean_note`、`audit_logs.subject_id`、審核（`rejected`、`review_note`、handle 鎖定、`set_creator_status`、`request_creator_review`、`admin_list_creators`）、違規訊息（`message_reviews`、`mark_message_reviewed`、`admin_list_flagged_messages`） |
| `supabase/migrations/20261128000200_admin_money.sql` | 收款帳戶、`payments.admin_note`、`collaboration_payouts`、`transition_collaboration`、`admin_lock_collab`、人工付款、撥款、看帳戶、平台取消、款項列表、總覽 |
| `supabase/tests/24_admin_core.test.sql`（48）、`25_admin_money.test.sql`（60） | pgTAP |
| `supabase/tests/03_collaborations.test.sql` | 直接 payout 前設旗標 |
| `src/lib/supabase/database.types.ts` | `npm run db:types` 產生（不手改） |
| `src/lib/admin/query.ts`、`notes.ts`、`errors.ts`、`view.ts`（各有 `.test.ts`） | 網址參數、說明驗證、錯誤文字、Row 轉換與規則 |
| `src/lib/admin/load.ts` | 伺服器端讀取（server-only） |
| `src/lib/payout-account.ts`（`.test.ts`） | 收款帳戶的解析、驗證、遮罩 |
| `src/lib/collabs/view.ts`、`src/lib/briefs/errors.ts`、`src/components/shell/nav.ts`、`NavIcon.tsx` | 事件文字、狀態已變、管理後台導覽 |
| `src/lib/auth/current-user.ts`、`post-login.ts`、`after-login.ts`、`routes.ts`、`dev-login.ts`、`src/app/auth/dev-login/route.ts`、`src/components/auth/DevLoginBox.tsx`、`src/app/onboarding/layout.tsx` | `requireAdmin`、登入後去向、需要登入的路徑、測試管理員 |
| `src/app/admin/actions.ts`（`.test.ts`） | 管理員的 Server Actions |
| `src/app/admin/layout.tsx`、`page.tsx`、`creators/page.tsx`、`collabs/page.tsx`、`collabs/[id]/page.tsx`、`payments/page.tsx`、`messages/page.tsx` | 頁面 |
| `src/components/admin/*` | 後台元件 |
| `src/app/creator/me/payout/page.tsx`、`PayoutAccountForm.tsx`、`actions.ts`（`.test.ts`） | 創作者的收款帳戶 |
| `src/app/creator/me/page.tsx`、`actions.ts`、`src/app/creator/StatusBanner.tsx`、`ResubmitButton.tsx` | 「收款帳戶」入口、退回原因、重新送審 |
| `README.md`、`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-22-foundation-followups.md` | 文件同步 |

---

### Task 1: 資料庫（一）：函式權限、創作者審核、違規訊息

**Files:**
- Create: `supabase/migrations/20261128000100_admin_core.sql`、`supabase/tests/24_admin_core.test.sql`
- Modify: `src/lib/supabase/database.types.ts`（產生）

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `public.is_admin()`、`public.my_role()`、`public.clean_text(text)`、`public.has_bidi(text)`、`public.audit_logs`、`public.creator_profiles`、`public.creator_verifications`、`public.messages`（`flagged_contact`）、`public.conversations`。
- Produces（後面的 task 依賴）：
  - `admin_actor() → text`、`admin_clean_note(p text, p_field text) → text`（內部）
  - `set_creator_status(p_user_id uuid, p_action text, p_reason text default null) → text`（authenticated、service_role）
  - `request_creator_review() → void`（authenticated）
  - `admin_list_creators(p_status text, p_query text, p_limit integer, p_offset integer) → table (user_id, handle, display_name, ig_username, follower_count, region, categories, status, review_note, verification_code, created_at, total_count)`
  - `mark_message_reviewed(p_message_id uuid) → boolean`、`admin_list_flagged_messages(p_view text, p_limit integer, p_offset integer) → table (id, conversation_id, body, created_at, sender_side, brand_id, brand_name, creator_id, creator_handle, creator_name, reviewed_at, total_count)`
  - 欄位 `audit_logs.subject_id`、`creator_profiles.review_note`；狀態 `rejected`；資料表 `message_reviews`

Run: `npx supabase test db`
Expected: `24_admin_core.test.sql` 失敗（`has_function_privilege` 的斷言不成立、`function public.set_creator_status(...) does not exist`）；其他檔全過。

- [ ] **Step 3: 寫 migration `supabase/migrations/20261128000100_admin_core.sql`**


```sql
-- 計畫 9：管理後台（一）權限收斂、創作者審核、違規訊息
-- 規則見 docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-27-admin-design.md。

-- 1. is_admin()／my_role() 收回 PUBLIC 與 anon 的 EXECUTE（followups §9）
--    authenticated 的 policy（RLS、storage、Realtime）會呼叫它們，所以再 grant 給 authenticated。
--    anon 會套用的 policy 只有 creator_profiles、creator_portfolio_items 兩條：拆成 anon 專用（不呼叫函式）與 authenticated。
revoke execute on function public.is_admin() from public, anon;
revoke execute on function public.my_role() from public, anon;
grant execute on function public.is_admin() to authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function public.my_role() to authenticated, service_role;

drop policy creator_profiles_select on public.creator_profiles;
create policy creator_profiles_select_anon on public.creator_profiles
  for select to anon
  using (status = 'approved');
create policy creator_profiles_select on public.creator_profiles
  for select to authenticated
  using (status = 'approved' or user_id = auth.uid() or public.is_admin());

drop policy creator_portfolio_items_select on public.creator_portfolio_items;
create policy creator_portfolio_items_select_anon on public.creator_portfolio_items
  for select to anon
  using (exists (
    select 1 from public.creator_profiles cp
     where cp.user_id = creator_portfolio_items.creator_id and cp.status = 'approved'));
create policy creator_portfolio_items_select on public.creator_portfolio_items
  for select to authenticated
  using (exists (
    select 1 from public.creator_profiles cp
     where cp.user_id = creator_portfolio_items.creator_id
       and (cp.status = 'approved' or cp.user_id = auth.uid() or public.is_admin())));

-- 2. 管理員身分（內部 helper，不授權給 API 角色）
--    管理員（is_admin）回傳 'admin'；以 service_role 執行的 README 備用 SQL 回傳 'system'；其他一律 42501 not admin。
create function public.admin_actor() returns text
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = '' as $$
begin
  if coalesce(auth.role(), '') = 'service_role'
     and coalesce(current_setting('role', true), '') = 'service_role' then
    return 'system';
  end if;
  if auth.uid() is not null and public.is_admin() then
    return 'admin';
  end if;
  raise exception 'not admin' using errcode = '42501';
end;
$$;
revoke execute on function public.admin_actor() from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;

-- 管理員備註／原因：clean_text 後 1–500 字、不能有雙向控制字元（src/lib/admin/notes.ts 的 ADMIN_NOTE_MAX 相同）
create function public.admin_clean_note(p text, p_field text) returns text
language plpgsql immutable set search_path = '' as $$
declare
  v text := nullif(public.clean_text(p), '');
begin
  if v is null or char_length(v) > 500 or public.has_bidi(v) then
    raise exception 'invalid %', p_field using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  return v;
end;
$$;
revoke execute on function public.admin_clean_note(text, text) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;

-- 3. audit_logs：管理員對「人」的動作（審核、看收款帳戶、檢視訊息）沒有合作案，記在 subject_id。
--    collaboration_id 為 null 的列只有管理員讀得到（audit_logs_select 的 exists 不成立）。
alter table public.audit_logs add column subject_id uuid;
create index audit_logs_subject_idx on public.audit_logs (subject_id, created_at) where subject_id is not null;

-- 4. 創作者審核：新增 rejected（退回，可修改後重新送審）與 review_note（退回／停權原因，給創作者本人看）
--    approved／pending 一律沒有 review_note，所以公開可讀的已審核列不會帶出原因。
alter table public.creator_profiles drop constraint creator_profiles_status_check;
alter table public.creator_profiles
  add constraint creator_profiles_status_check
    check (status in ('pending', 'approved', 'suspended', 'rejected')),
  add column review_note text,
  add constraint creator_profiles_review_note
    check (review_note is null
           or (status in ('rejected', 'suspended') and char_length(review_note) between 1 and 500));

-- handle 鎖定：原本「第一次離開 pending 就鎖定」，退回（rejected）不鎖，只有 approved／suspended 才鎖。
-- 其餘與 20261123000100_accounts.sql 相同。
create or replace function public.creator_profiles_lock_handle() returns trigger
language plpgsql set search_path = '' as $$
declare
  v_admin boolean := coalesce(current_setting('kollink.allow_handle_change', true), '') = 'on'
    and current_user in ('postgres', 'supabase_admin');
begin
  if tg_op = 'INSERT' then
    -- 傳入的 handle_locked_at 一律忽略，由 status 決定
    new.handle_locked_at := case when new.status in ('approved', 'suspended') then now() else null end;
    return new;
  end if;

  if not v_admin then
    if new.user_id is distinct from old.user_id then
      raise exception 'handle locked after approval' using errcode = '42501';
    end if;
    if old.handle_locked_at is not null then
      if new.handle is distinct from old.handle
         or new.handle_locked_at is distinct from old.handle_locked_at then
        raise exception 'handle locked after approval' using errcode = '42501';
      end if;
      return new;
    end if;
    -- 未鎖定時不能自行設定 handle_locked_at
    if new.handle_locked_at is not null then
      raise exception 'handle locked after approval' using errcode = '42501';
    end if;
  elsif old.handle_locked_at is not null then
    return new;
  end if;

  -- 第一次審核通過（或停權）就鎖定
  if new.status in ('approved', 'suspended') and new.handle_locked_at is null then
    new.handle_locked_at := now();
  end if;
  return new;
end;
$$;

-- 管理員改創作者狀態（followups §3、§9 的 set_creator_status）
--   approve：pending／rejected → approved
--   reject：pending → rejected（原因必填，創作者看得到）
--   suspend：approved → suspended（原因必填，創作者看得到；對話自動暫停，計畫 7）
--   unsuspend：suspended → approved
-- 寫 audit（action = creator_<動作>、subject_id = 創作者、note = 原因）。回傳新狀態。
create function public.set_creator_status(p_user_id uuid, p_action text, p_reason text default null)
returns text
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare
  v_actor text := public.admin_actor();
  v_reason text;
  v_old text;
  v_new text;
begin
  if p_action is null or p_action not in ('approve', 'reject', 'suspend', 'unsuspend') then
    raise exception 'invalid action' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if p_action in ('reject', 'suspend') then
    v_reason := public.admin_clean_note(p_reason, 'reason');
  elsif nullif(public.clean_text(p_reason), '') is not null then
    v_reason := public.admin_clean_note(p_reason, 'reason');
  end if;

  select cp.status into v_old from public.creator_profiles cp where cp.user_id = p_user_id for update;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'creator not found' using errcode = 'P0002';
  end if;
  v_new := case
    when p_action = 'approve' and v_old in ('pending', 'rejected') then 'approved'
    when p_action = 'reject' and v_old = 'pending' then 'rejected'
    when p_action = 'suspend' and v_old = 'approved' then 'suspended'
    when p_action = 'unsuspend' and v_old = 'suspended' then 'approved'
  end;
  if v_new is null then
    raise exception 'creator status changed' using errcode = '55000';
  end if;

  update public.creator_profiles
     set status = v_new,
         review_note = case when v_new in ('rejected', 'suspended') then v_reason end
   where user_id = p_user_id;

  insert into public.audit_logs (collaboration_id, actor_id, actor_kind, action, subject_id, note)
  values (null, case when v_actor = 'admin' then auth.uid() end, v_actor, 'creator_' || p_action, p_user_id, v_reason);
  return v_new;
end;
$$;
revoke execute on function public.set_creator_status(uuid, text, text) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function public.set_creator_status(uuid, text, text) to authenticated, service_role;

-- 創作者被退回後，修正完自己按「重新送審」：rejected → pending，清掉原因（驗證碼不變）。
create function public.request_creator_review() returns void
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare
  v_uid uuid := auth.uid();
  v_old text;
begin
  if v_uid is null then
    raise exception 'not authenticated' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  select cp.status into v_old from public.creator_profiles cp where cp.user_id = v_uid for update;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'not a creator' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if v_old <> 'rejected' then
    raise exception 'creator status changed' using errcode = '55000';
  end if;
  update public.creator_profiles set status = 'pending', review_note = null where user_id = v_uid;
  insert into public.audit_logs (collaboration_id, actor_id, actor_kind, action, subject_id)
  values (null, v_uid, 'creator', 'creator_resubmit', v_uid);
end;
$$;
revoke execute on function public.request_creator_review() from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function public.request_creator_review() to authenticated;

-- 審核列表：p_status = pending｜rejected｜approved｜suspended｜all；p_query 比對 handle、IG、名稱（不分大小寫、子字串）。
-- 待審核依申請時間由舊到新（先來先審），其他由新到舊。總數放在 total_count。
create function public.admin_list_creators(p_status text, p_query text, p_limit integer, p_offset integer)
returns table (
  user_id uuid, handle text, display_name text, ig_username text, follower_count integer, region text,
  categories text[], status text, review_note text, verification_code text, created_at timestamptz,
  total_count bigint
)
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = '' as $$
#variable_conflict use_column
declare
  v_q text := nullif(left(public.clean_text(p_query), 50), '');
  v_like text;
begin
  perform public.admin_actor();
  if p_status is null or p_status not in ('pending', 'rejected', 'approved', 'suspended', 'all') then
    raise exception 'invalid status' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  v_like := '%' || replace(replace(replace(coalesce(v_q, ''), '\', '\\'), '%', '\%'), '_', '\_') || '%';
  return query
  select cp.user_id, cp.handle, p.display_name, cp.ig_username, cp.follower_count, cp.region,
         cp.categories, cp.status, cp.review_note, v.code, cp.created_at,
         count(*) over ()
    from public.creator_profiles cp
    join public.profiles p on p.id = cp.user_id
    left join public.creator_verifications v on v.user_id = cp.user_id
   where (p_status = 'all' or cp.status = p_status)
     and (v_q is null or cp.handle ilike v_like or cp.ig_username ilike v_like or p.display_name ilike v_like)
   order by case when p_status = 'pending' then cp.created_at end asc,
            cp.created_at desc, cp.user_id
   limit least(greatest(coalesce(p_limit, 50), 1), 100)
  offset greatest(coalesce(p_offset, 0), 0);
end;
$$;
revoke execute on function public.admin_list_creators(text, text, integer, integer) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function public.admin_list_creators(text, text, integer, integer) to authenticated;

-- 5. 違規訊息（flagged_contact）的檢視紀錄。只有管理員讀得到；寫入只經由 mark_message_reviewed。
create table public.message_reviews (
  message_id uuid primary key references public.messages (id) on delete cascade,
  reviewed_by uuid,
  reviewed_at timestamptz not null default now()
);
alter table public.message_reviews enable row level security;
create policy message_reviews_admin_select on public.message_reviews
  for select to authenticated using (public.is_admin());
revoke all on public.message_reviews from anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant select on public.message_reviews to authenticated, service_role;

create index messages_flagged_idx on public.messages (created_at desc) where flagged_contact;

-- 標記已檢視：只能標記有 flagged_contact 的訊息；重複標記不報錯（回傳 false）。第一次標記寫 audit（message_reviewed）。
create function public.mark_message_reviewed(p_message_id uuid) returns boolean
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare
  v_actor text := public.admin_actor();
  v_sender uuid;
  v_flagged boolean;
  v_inserted uuid;
begin
  select m.sender_id, m.flagged_contact into v_sender, v_flagged from public.messages m where m.id = p_message_id;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'message not found' using errcode = 'P0002';
  end if;
  if not v_flagged then
    raise exception 'message not flagged' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  insert into public.message_reviews (message_id, reviewed_by)
  values (p_message_id, case when v_actor = 'admin' then auth.uid() end)
  on conflict (message_id) do nothing
  returning message_id into v_inserted;
  if v_inserted is null then
    return false;
  end if;
  insert into public.audit_logs (collaboration_id, actor_id, actor_kind, action, subject_id, note)
  values (null, case when v_actor = 'admin' then auth.uid() end, v_actor, 'message_reviewed', v_sender,
          'message ' || p_message_id::text);
  return true;
end;
$$;
revoke execute on function public.mark_message_reviewed(uuid) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function public.mark_message_reviewed(uuid) to authenticated, service_role;

-- 違規訊息列表：p_view = open（未檢視）｜reviewed｜all；由新到舊。
create function public.admin_list_flagged_messages(p_view text, p_limit integer, p_offset integer)
returns table (
  id uuid, conversation_id uuid, body text, created_at timestamptz, sender_side text,
  brand_id uuid, brand_name text, creator_id uuid, creator_handle text, creator_name text,
  reviewed_at timestamptz, total_count bigint
)
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = '' as $$
#variable_conflict use_column
begin
  perform public.admin_actor();
  if p_view is null or p_view not in ('open', 'reviewed', 'all') then
    raise exception 'invalid view' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  return query
  select m.id, m.conversation_id, m.body, m.created_at,
         case when m.sender_id = cv.brand_id then 'brand' else 'creator' end,
         cv.brand_id, bp.brand_name, cv.creator_id, cp.handle, p.display_name,
         r.reviewed_at, count(*) over ()
    from public.messages m
    join public.conversations cv on cv.id = m.conversation_id
    left join public.brand_profiles bp on bp.user_id = cv.brand_id
    left join public.creator_profiles cp on cp.user_id = cv.creator_id
    left join public.profiles p on p.id = cv.creator_id
    left join public.message_reviews r on r.message_id = m.id
   where m.flagged_contact
     and (p_view = 'all' or (p_view = 'open') = (r.message_id is null))
   order by m.created_at desc, m.id
   limit least(greatest(coalesce(p_limit, 50), 1), 100)
  offset greatest(coalesce(p_offset, 0), 0);
end;
$$;
revoke execute on function public.admin_list_flagged_messages(text, integer, integer) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function public.admin_list_flagged_messages(text, integer, integer) to authenticated;
```

### Task 2: 資料庫（二）：收款帳戶、人工付款、撥款、平台取消、款項列表

**Files:**
- Create: `supabase/migrations/20261128000200_admin_money.sql`、`supabase/tests/25_admin_money.test.sql`
- Modify: `supabase/tests/03_collaborations.test.sql`、`src/lib/supabase/database.types.ts`（產生）

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1 的 `admin_actor()`、`admin_clean_note()`、`audit_logs.subject_id`；`public.record_payment_success(text, integer, text, jsonb, text)`（計畫 8）、`public.payments`、`public.payment_anomalies`、`public.collaborations`、`public.message_reviews`。
- Produces（後面的 task 依賴）：
  - `set_my_payout_account(p_bank_code text, p_branch_code text, p_account_number text, p_account_name text) → void`（authenticated）
  - `admin_record_offline_payment(p_collab_id uuid, p_note text) → text`、`admin_record_lost_payment(p_merchant_trade_no text, p_provider_trade_no text, p_amount_twd integer, p_env text, p_note text) → text`（`paid`｜`already_paid`｜`paid_refund_required`｜`anomaly`）
  - `admin_mark_payout(p_collab_id uuid, p_note text) → void`、`admin_get_payout_account(p_collab_id uuid) → table (bank_code, branch_code, account_number, account_name, updated_at)`
  - `admin_cancel_collaboration(p_collab_id uuid, p_reason text) → integer`（標記需退款的筆數）
  - `admin_list_collaborations(p_status, p_query, p_limit, p_offset)`、`admin_list_payments(p_view, p_limit, p_offset, p_collab_id default null)`、`admin_list_payouts(p_limit, p_offset)`、`admin_overview()`
  - 資料表 `creator_payout_accounts`（authenticated 讀不到 `account_number`）、`collaboration_payouts`；欄位 `payments.admin_note`（authenticated 讀不到）
  - `transition_collaboration`：`mark_paid` 允許 admin；`payout` 需要 `kollink.paying_out`；新動作 `admin_cancel`；新事件 `refund_scheduled`

```sql
  $$select public.admin_record_lost_payment('KL261127P9LOST000001', '2411270000000001', 3000, 'test', '已確認')$$,
  '22023', 'invalid env', '環境只能是 stage／prod');

  'P0002', 'payment not found', '找不到付款單');

  '22023', 'not an online payment', 'MAN 單不能用這條路');
select is(
  public.admin_record_lost_payment('KL261127P9LOST000001', '2411270000000001', 3001, 'stage', '金額打錯'),
  'anomaly', '金額不符：記錄為付款異常');
select is(
  public.admin_record_lost_payment('KL261127P9LOST000001', '2411270000000001', 3000, 'stage', '綠界後台確認 11/27 成功'),
  'paid', '金額相符：標記已付款');

  (select status || ',' || provider_env || ',' || provider_trade_no || ',' || admin_note
     from public.admin_list_payments('paid', 50, 0, '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000ac2')),
  'paid,stage,2411270000000001,綠界後台確認 11/27 成功', '記下環境、交易編號、說明');

select set_config('request.jwt.claims', '{"sub":"00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000a28","role":"authenticated"}', true);
select throws_ok(
  $$select public.admin_record_offline_payment('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000ac7', '自己付')$$,
  '42501', 'admin is participant', '管理員不能標記自己品牌的合作案');

-- ===== 4. 撥款 =====
select set_config('request.jwt.claims', '{"sub":"00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000a29","role":"authenticated"}', true);
select is(
  (select account_number from public.admin_get_payout_account('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000ac3')),
  '123456785678', '撥款畫面看得到完整帳號');
select is(
  (select account_number from public.admin_get_payout_account('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000ac3')),
  '123456785678', '再看一次');
select is(
  (select count(*)::int from public.audit_logs
    where action = 'payout_account_viewed' and subject_id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000a21'),
  1, '10 分鐘內只記一筆查看紀錄');
select is(
  (select count(*)::int from public.admin_get_payout_account('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000ac1')),
  0, '不是待撥款的合作案不回傳帳號');
select throws_ok(
  $$select public.transition_collaboration('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000ac3', 'payout')$$,
  '22023', null, '不能直接 payout（要經由 admin_mark_payout）');
select throws_ok(
  $$select public.admin_mark_payout('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000ac4', '已轉帳')$$,
  '55000', 'test payment cannot be paid out', '測試付款不能撥款');
select throws_ok(
  $$select public.admin_mark_payout('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000ac5', '已轉帳')$$,
  '55000', 'payout account missing', '創作者沒有收款帳戶');
select throws_ok(
  $$select public.admin_mark_payout('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000ac3', '')$$,
  '22023', 'invalid note', '轉帳說明必填');
select lives_ok(
  $$select public.admin_mark_payout('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000ac3', '11/28 國泰網銀轉帳，交易序號 778899')$$,
  '撥款');
select is(
  (select status::text from public.collaborations where id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000ac3'),
  'completed', '結案');
select is(
  (select account_last4 || ',' || amount_twd || ',' || actor_kind || ',' || transfer_note
     from public.collaboration_payouts where collaboration_id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000ac3'),
  '5678,5000,admin,11/28 國泰網銀轉帳，交易序號 778899', '撥款紀錄留下帳戶末四碼與轉帳說明');
select ok(
  (select note is null and actor_kind = 'admin' from public.audit_logs
    where collaboration_id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000ac3' and action = 'payout'),
  'payout 的 audit 不放轉帳說明（參與者看得到）');
select throws_ok(
  $$select public.admin_mark_payout('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000ac3', '再一次')$$,
  '55000', 'collaboration not awaiting payout', '不能重複撥款');

-- ===== 5. 平台取消 =====
select throws_ok(
  $$select public.admin_cancel_collaboration('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000ac6', '')$$,
  '22023', 'invalid reason', '原因必填');
select throws_ok(
  $$select public.transition_collaboration('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000ac6', 'admin_cancel', '直接取消')$$,
  '22023', null, '不能直接 admin_cancel（要經由 admin_cancel_collaboration）');
select is(
  public.admin_cancel_collaboration('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000ac6', '創作者無法交稿，雙方同意取消'),
  1, '已付款的合作案取消：標記 1 筆需退款');
select ok(
  (select c.status = 'cancelled' and p.refund_required
     from public.collaborations c join public.payments p on p.collaboration_id = c.id
    where c.id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000ac6'),
  '狀態取消、款項需退款');
select is(
  public.admin_cancel_collaboration('a9c8a9c8-0000-0000-0000-000000000ac8', '品牌要求取消'),
  0, '沒有付款的合作案取消：不需退款');
select throws_ok(
  $$select public.admin_cancel_collaboration('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000ac3', '太晚了')$$,
  '55000', 'collaboration already closed', '已結案不能取消');

-- ===== 6. 列表與總覽 =====
select is(
  (select count(*)::int from public.admin_list_collaborations('all', 'a9c8a9c8', 50, 0)),
  1, '用案號找合作案');
select is(
  (select count(*)::int from public.admin_list_collaborations('awaiting_payout', '九號', 50, 0)),
  2, '依狀態與案名篩選');
select ok(
  (select test_paid from public.admin_list_collaborations('all', '九號測試付款', 50, 0)),
  '列表標出測試付款');
select throws_ok(
  $$select * from public.admin_list_collaborations('bogus', null, 50, 0)$$,
  '22023', 'invalid status', '不認得的狀態');
select is(
  (select count(*)::int from public.admin_list_payments('refund', 50, 0, '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000ac6')),
  1, '需退款列表');
select is(
  (select coalesce(bank_code, 'null') || ',' || test_paid from public.admin_list_payouts(100, 0)
    where id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000ac5'),
  'null,false', '待撥款列表標出沒有收款帳戶');
select ok((select awaiting_payout >= 2 from public.admin_overview()), '總覽數字');
reset role;

-- ===== 7. service_role（README 備用 SQL） =====
set local role service_role;
select set_config('request.jwt.claims', '{"role":"service_role"}', true);
select is(
  public.admin_record_offline_payment('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000ac9', '備用 SQL'),
  'paid', 'service_role 也可以人工標記已付款');
reset role;
select is(
  (select actor_kind || ',' || coalesce(actor_id::text, 'null') from public.audit_logs
    where collaboration_id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000ac9' and action = 'mark_paid'),
  'system,null', 'service_role 的 actor 是 system');

select * from finish();
rollback;
```

找到：

```sql
  'completed'::public.collab_status, '管理員撥款，結案');
```

換成：


- [ ] **Step 3: 跑測試確認失敗**

Run: `npx supabase test db`
Expected: `25_admin_money.test.sql` 失敗（`function public.set_my_payout_account(...) does not exist`）；`03` 仍然全過（舊的 `transition_collaboration` 不看旗標）。

- [ ] **Step 4: 寫 migration `supabase/migrations/20261128000200_admin_money.sql`**

`transition_collaboration` 是整段重寫：內容與 `20261125000500_collab_text_guards.sql` 相同，只改三處（`mark_paid` 的 `v_actor in ('system', 'admin')`、`payout` 加 `kollink.paying_out`、新增 `admin_cancel` 一個 `when`）。寫完用 `diff` 對一次，確認沒有其他差異。


```sql
-- 計畫 9：管理後台（二）收款帳戶、人工標記已付款、撥款、平台取消、款項列表
-- 規則見 docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-27-admin-design.md。

-- 1. 創作者收款帳戶（敏感資料）：本人與管理員可讀，但 account_number 不開放任何 API 角色直接讀；
--    本人與列表只看得到末四碼（account_last4），完整帳號只能經由 admin_get_payout_account（撥款畫面、寫 audit）。
--    寫入只經由 set_my_payout_account。規則與 src/lib/payout-account.ts 相同。
create table public.creator_payout_accounts (
  user_id uuid primary key references public.creator_profiles (user_id) on delete cascade,
  bank_code text not null check (bank_code ~ '^[0-9]{3}$'),
  branch_code text check (branch_code is null or branch_code ~ '^[0-9]{4}$'),
  account_number text not null check (account_number ~ '^[0-9]{7,16}$'),
  account_last4 text generated always as (right(account_number, 4)) stored,
  account_name text not null check (char_length(account_name) between 1 and 40),
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now()
);
alter table public.creator_payout_accounts enable row level security;
create policy creator_payout_accounts_select on public.creator_payout_accounts
  for select to authenticated using (user_id = auth.uid() or public.is_admin());
revoke all on public.creator_payout_accounts from anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant select (user_id, bank_code, branch_code, account_last4, account_name, updated_at)
  on public.creator_payout_accounts to authenticated;

-- 創作者自己設定（任何審核狀態都可以先填）。輸入的空白與「-」會去掉。每次修改寫 audit（payout_account_updated）。
create function public.set_my_payout_account(
  p_bank_code text, p_branch_code text, p_account_number text, p_account_name text
) returns void
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare
  v_uid uuid := auth.uid();
  v_bank text := regexp_replace(coalesce(p_bank_code, ''), '[\s-]', '', 'g');
  v_branch text := nullif(regexp_replace(coalesce(p_branch_code, ''), '[\s-]', '', 'g'), '');
  v_account text := regexp_replace(coalesce(p_account_number, ''), '[\s-]', '', 'g');
  v_name text := nullif(public.clean_text(p_account_name), '');
begin
  if v_uid is null then
    raise exception 'not authenticated' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if not exists (select 1 from public.creator_profiles cp where cp.user_id = v_uid) then
    raise exception 'not a creator' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if v_bank !~ '^[0-9]{3}$' then
    raise exception 'invalid bank_code' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if v_branch is not null and v_branch !~ '^[0-9]{4}$' then
    raise exception 'invalid branch_code' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if v_account !~ '^[0-9]{7,16}$' then
    raise exception 'invalid account_number' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if v_name is null or char_length(v_name) > 40 or public.has_bidi(v_name) then
    raise exception 'invalid account_name' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;

  insert into public.creator_payout_accounts (user_id, bank_code, branch_code, account_number, account_name)
  values (v_uid, v_bank, v_branch, v_account, v_name)
  on conflict (user_id) do update
    set bank_code = excluded.bank_code, branch_code = excluded.branch_code,
        account_number = excluded.account_number, account_name = excluded.account_name, updated_at = now();

  insert into public.audit_logs (collaboration_id, actor_id, actor_kind, action, subject_id, note)
  values (null, v_uid, 'creator', 'payout_account_updated', v_uid, v_bank || ' ****' || right(v_account, 4));
end;
$$;
revoke execute on function public.set_my_payout_account(text, text, text, text) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function public.set_my_payout_account(text, text, text, text) to authenticated;

-- 2. payments.admin_note：人工標記已付款時管理員寫的說明（authenticated 的欄位權限不含這欄，與 refund_note 相同）
alter table public.payments
  add column admin_note text check (admin_note is null or char_length(admin_note) between 1 and 500);

-- 3. 撥款紀錄：撥款當下的帳戶快照（只留末四碼）與轉帳說明。只有管理員讀得到；寫入只經由 admin_mark_payout。
create table public.collaboration_payouts (
  collaboration_id uuid primary key references public.collaborations (id),
  amount_twd integer not null check (amount_twd > 0),
  bank_code text not null,
  branch_code text,
  account_last4 text not null,
  account_name text not null,
  transfer_note text not null check (char_length(transfer_note) between 1 and 500),
  actor_kind text not null check (actor_kind in ('admin', 'system')),
  paid_out_by uuid,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);
alter table public.collaboration_payouts enable row level security;
create policy collaboration_payouts_admin_select on public.collaboration_payouts
  for select to authenticated using (public.is_admin());
revoke all on public.collaboration_payouts from anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant select on public.collaboration_payouts to authenticated, service_role;

-- 4. transition_collaboration：與 20261125000500_collab_text_guards.sql 相同，只改三處
--    a) mark_paid：管理員（經由 admin_record_* 呼叫 record_payment_success）也可以，仍然只能在 kollink.recording_payment 旗標下
--    b) payout：只能經由 admin_mark_payout（kollink.paying_out 旗標），不能直接呼叫跳過測試付款與收款帳戶檢查
--    c) admin_cancel：平台取消（爭議處理），任何未結束的狀態，只能經由 admin_cancel_collaboration（kollink.admin_cancelling 旗標），原因必填
create or replace function public.transition_collaboration(
  p_collab_id uuid,
  p_action text,
  p_note text default null,
  p_published_url text default null
) returns public.collab_status
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare
  c public.collaborations%rowtype;
  v_uid uuid := auth.uid();
  v_actor text;
  v_to public.collab_status;
  -- 與 src/lib/validation/collab.ts 的 NOTE_MAX（1000）、POST_URL_MAX（500）、hasBadChar 一致
  v_note text := nullif(public.clean_text(p_note), '');
  v_url text := nullif(public.clean_text(p_published_url), '');
begin
  if v_note is not null and (public.has_bidi(v_note) or char_length(v_note) > 1000) then
    raise exception 'invalid note' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if v_url is not null and (public.has_bidi(v_url) or char_length(v_url) > 500) then
    raise exception 'invalid published_url' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;

  select * into c from public.collaborations where id = p_collab_id for update;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'collaboration % not found', p_collab_id using errcode = 'P0002';
  end if;

  -- 身分優先順序：system > brand > creator > admin（參與者永遠以參與者身分行動）
  if coalesce(auth.role(), '') = 'service_role'
     and coalesce(current_setting('role', true), '') = 'service_role' then
    v_actor := 'system';
  elsif v_uid = c.brand_id then
    v_actor := 'brand';
  elsif v_uid = c.creator_id then
    v_actor := 'creator';
  elsif public.is_admin() then
    v_actor := 'admin';
  else
    raise exception 'not a participant of collaboration %', p_collab_id using errcode = '42501';
  end if;

  v_to := case
    when c.status = 'invited' and p_action = 'accept' and v_actor = 'creator'
      then 'awaiting_payment'::public.collab_status
    when c.status = 'invited' and p_action = 'decline' and v_actor = 'creator'
      then 'declined'::public.collab_status
    when c.status in ('invited', 'awaiting_payment') and p_action = 'cancel' and v_actor = 'brand'
      then 'cancelled'::public.collab_status
    when c.status = 'awaiting_payment' and p_action = 'mark_paid' and v_actor in ('system', 'admin')
         and coalesce(current_setting('kollink.recording_payment', true), '') = 'on'
      then 'awaiting_submission'::public.collab_status
    when c.status = 'awaiting_submission' and p_action = 'submit' and v_actor = 'creator'
         and coalesce(current_setting('kollink.submitting_deliverables', true), '') = 'on'
      then 'in_review'::public.collab_status
    when c.status = 'in_review' and p_action = 'approve' and v_actor = 'brand'
      then 'awaiting_publish'::public.collab_status
    when c.status = 'in_review' and p_action = 'auto_approve' and v_actor = 'system'
         and c.review_due_at <= now()
      then 'awaiting_publish'::public.collab_status
    when c.status = 'in_review' and p_action = 'request_changes' and v_actor = 'brand'
         and v_note is not null
      then 'awaiting_submission'::public.collab_status
    when c.status = 'awaiting_publish' and p_action = 'publish' and v_actor = 'creator'
         and v_url ~ '^https://[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\.[A-Za-z]{2,}/\S+$'
      then 'awaiting_payout'::public.collab_status
    when c.status = 'awaiting_payout' and p_action = 'payout' and v_actor in ('admin', 'system')
         and coalesce(current_setting('kollink.paying_out', true), '') = 'on'
      then 'completed'::public.collab_status
    when c.status in ('invited', 'awaiting_payment', 'awaiting_submission', 'in_review',
                      'awaiting_publish', 'awaiting_payout')
         and p_action = 'admin_cancel' and v_actor in ('admin', 'system')
         and coalesce(current_setting('kollink.admin_cancelling', true), '') = 'on'
         and v_note is not null
      then 'cancelled'::public.collab_status
    else null
  end;

  if v_to is null then
    raise exception 'invalid transition: % from % by %', p_action, c.status, v_actor
      using errcode = '22023';
  end if;

  perform set_config('kollink.in_transition', 'on', true);
  update public.collaborations
  set status = v_to,
      updated_at = now(),
      review_due_at = case when v_to = 'in_review' then now() + interval '3 days' else review_due_at end,
      published_url = case when v_to = 'awaiting_payout' then v_url else published_url end
  where id = c.id;
  perform set_config('kollink.in_transition', 'off', true);

  insert into public.audit_logs (collaboration_id, actor_id, actor_kind, action, from_status, to_status, note)
  values (c.id, case when v_actor = 'system' then null else v_uid end, v_actor, p_action, c.status, v_to, v_note);

  return v_to;
end;
$$;
revoke execute on function public.transition_collaboration(uuid, text, text, text) from public, anon;
grant execute on function public.transition_collaboration(uuid, text, text, text) to authenticated, service_role;

-- 管理員是合作案的參與者時，不能用管理員身分處理自己的案子（與 transition_collaboration 的身分優先相同）。
create function public.admin_lock_collab(p_collab_id uuid, p_actor text) returns public.collaborations
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare
  c public.collaborations%rowtype;
begin
  select * into c from public.collaborations where id = p_collab_id for update;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'collaboration not found' using errcode = 'P0002';
  end if;
  if p_actor = 'admin' and auth.uid() in (c.brand_id, c.creator_id) then
    raise exception 'admin is participant' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  return c;
end;
$$;
revoke execute on function public.admin_lock_collab(uuid, text) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;

-- 5. 人工標記已付款：線下付款／超過線上付款上限（取代 README 的 MAN 單 SQL）
--    建一張 MAN 開頭的付款單（金額 = 合作案報價、admin_note = 說明），再呼叫 record_payment_success。
--    回傳 record_payment_success 的結果（正常是 paid）。
create function public.admin_record_offline_payment(p_collab_id uuid, p_note text) returns text
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare
  v_actor text := public.admin_actor();
  v_note text := public.admin_clean_note(p_note, 'note');
  c public.collaborations%rowtype;
  v_no text;
begin
  c := public.admin_lock_collab(p_collab_id, v_actor);
  if c.status <> 'awaiting_payment' then
    raise exception 'collaboration not awaiting payment' using errcode = '55000';
  end if;
  -- MAN + 17 位大寫十六進位 = 20 字（payments.merchant_trade_no 上限 20、英數字）
  v_no := 'MAN' || upper(left(replace(gen_random_uuid()::text, '-', ''), 17));
  insert into public.payments (collaboration_id, provider, merchant_trade_no, amount_twd, created_by, admin_note)
  values (c.id, 'ecpay', v_no, c.amount_twd, case when v_actor = 'admin' then auth.uid() end, v_note);
  return public.record_payment_success(v_no, c.amount_twd, 'MANUAL-' || v_no, '{"source":"admin"}'::jsonb, null);
end;
$$;
revoke execute on function public.admin_record_offline_payment(uuid, text) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function public.admin_record_offline_payment(uuid, text) to authenticated, service_role;

-- 綠界已扣款、但沒收到通知（已在綠界廠商後台確認）：用既有的 KL 付款單呼叫 record_payment_success。
-- p_env 是建單時的綠界環境（App 由 ECPAY_ENV 帶入；stage 的款項之後不能撥款）。
-- 回傳 paid｜already_paid｜paid_refund_required｜anomaly（金額與付款單不符時記錄為付款異常）。
create function public.admin_record_lost_payment(
  p_merchant_trade_no text, p_provider_trade_no text, p_amount_twd integer, p_env text, p_note text
) returns text
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare
  v_actor text := public.admin_actor();
  v_note text := public.admin_clean_note(p_note, 'note');
  v_ptn text := btrim(coalesce(p_provider_trade_no, ''));
  p public.payments%rowtype;
begin
  if p_env is null or p_env not in ('stage', 'prod') then
    raise exception 'invalid env' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if v_ptn !~ '^[A-Za-z0-9]{1,20}$' then
    raise exception 'invalid provider_trade_no' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if p_amount_twd is null or p_amount_twd <= 0 then
    raise exception 'invalid amount' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  select * into p from public.payments where merchant_trade_no = p_merchant_trade_no;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'payment not found' using errcode = 'P0002';
  end if;
  if p.merchant_trade_no !~ '^KL' then
    raise exception 'not an online payment' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  perform public.admin_lock_collab(p.collaboration_id, v_actor);
  if p.status <> 'paid' then
    update public.payments set admin_note = v_note where id = p.id;
  end if;
  return public.record_payment_success(p.merchant_trade_no, p_amount_twd, v_ptn, '{"source":"admin"}'::jsonb, p_env);
end;
$$;
revoke execute on function public.admin_record_lost_payment(text, text, integer, text, text) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function public.admin_record_lost_payment(text, text, integer, text, text) to authenticated, service_role;

-- 6. 撥款（awaiting_payout → completed）：管理員在平台外轉帳後標記。
--    拒絕：測試付款（provider_env = stage）、沒有已付款的款項、有待退款的款項、創作者沒有收款帳戶。
--    在 collaboration_payouts 留下帳戶快照（末四碼）與轉帳說明；audit 的 payout 列不放說明（參與者看得到）。
create function public.admin_mark_payout(p_collab_id uuid, p_note text) returns void
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare
  v_actor text := public.admin_actor();
  v_note text := public.admin_clean_note(p_note, 'note');
  c public.collaborations%rowtype;
  a public.creator_payout_accounts%rowtype;
begin
  c := public.admin_lock_collab(p_collab_id, v_actor);
  if c.status <> 'awaiting_payout' then
    raise exception 'collaboration not awaiting payout' using errcode = '55000';
  end if;
  if exists (select 1 from public.payments p
              where p.collaboration_id = c.id and p.status = 'paid' and not p.refund_required
                and p.provider_env = 'stage') then
    raise exception 'test payment cannot be paid out' using errcode = '55000';
  end if;
  if not exists (select 1 from public.payments p
                  where p.collaboration_id = c.id and p.status = 'paid' and not p.refund_required
                    and p.refunded_at is null) then
    raise exception 'no paid payment' using errcode = '55000';
  end if;
  if exists (select 1 from public.payments p where p.collaboration_id = c.id and p.refund_required) then
    raise exception 'refund pending' using errcode = '55000';
  end if;
  select * into a from public.creator_payout_accounts where user_id = c.creator_id;
  if not found then
    raise exception 'payout account missing' using errcode = '55000';
  end if;

  insert into public.collaboration_payouts
    (collaboration_id, amount_twd, bank_code, branch_code, account_last4, account_name, transfer_note, actor_kind, paid_out_by)
  values
    (c.id, c.amount_twd, a.bank_code, a.branch_code, a.account_last4, a.account_name, v_note, v_actor,
     case when v_actor = 'admin' then auth.uid() end);

  perform set_config('kollink.paying_out', 'on', true);
  perform public.transition_collaboration(c.id, 'payout');
  perform set_config('kollink.paying_out', 'off', true);
end;
$$;
revoke execute on function public.admin_mark_payout(uuid, text) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function public.admin_mark_payout(uuid, text) to authenticated, service_role;

-- 撥款畫面看完整帳號：只在合作案 awaiting_payout 時回傳；每位管理員對同一位創作者 10 分鐘內只記一筆 audit（payout_account_viewed）。
create function public.admin_get_payout_account(p_collab_id uuid)
returns table (bank_code text, branch_code text, account_number text, account_name text, updated_at timestamptz)
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
#variable_conflict use_column
declare
  v_actor text := public.admin_actor();
  v_uid uuid := auth.uid();
  c public.collaborations%rowtype;
begin
  select * into c from public.collaborations where id = p_collab_id;
  if not found or c.status <> 'awaiting_payout' then
    return;
  end if;
  if v_actor = 'admin' and v_uid in (c.brand_id, c.creator_id) then
    raise exception 'admin is participant' using errcode = '42501';
  end if;
  if not exists (select 1 from public.creator_payout_accounts a where a.user_id = c.creator_id) then
    return;
  end if;
  if not exists (select 1 from public.audit_logs l
                  where l.subject_id = c.creator_id and l.action = 'payout_account_viewed'
                    and l.actor_id is not distinct from (case when v_actor = 'admin' then v_uid end)
                    and l.created_at > now() - interval '10 minutes') then
    insert into public.audit_logs (collaboration_id, actor_id, actor_kind, action, subject_id, note)
    values (null, case when v_actor = 'admin' then v_uid end, v_actor, 'payout_account_viewed', c.creator_id,
            'case ' || left(c.id::text, 8));
  end if;
  return query
  select a.bank_code, a.branch_code, a.account_number, a.account_name, a.updated_at
    from public.creator_payout_accounts a where a.user_id = c.creator_id;
end;
$$;
revoke execute on function public.admin_get_payout_account(uuid) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function public.admin_get_payout_account(uuid) to authenticated, service_role;

-- 7. 平台取消（爭議處理）：任何未結束的狀態 → cancelled，原因必填（雙方在事件裡看得到）。
--    已付款且還沒退款的款項一律標記需退款（refund_required），並寫一筆 refund_scheduled。回傳標記了幾筆。
create function public.admin_cancel_collaboration(p_collab_id uuid, p_reason text) returns integer
language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare
  v_actor text := public.admin_actor();
  v_reason text := public.admin_clean_note(p_reason, 'reason');
  c public.collaborations%rowtype;
  v_n integer;
begin
  c := public.admin_lock_collab(p_collab_id, v_actor);
  if c.status in ('completed', 'declined', 'cancelled') then
    raise exception 'collaboration already closed' using errcode = '55000';
  end if;
  perform set_config('kollink.admin_cancelling', 'on', true);
  perform public.transition_collaboration(c.id, 'admin_cancel', v_reason);
  perform set_config('kollink.admin_cancelling', 'off', true);

  update public.payments p set refund_required = true
   where p.collaboration_id = c.id and p.status = 'paid' and not p.refund_required and p.refunded_at is null;
  get diagnostics v_n = row_count;
  if v_n > 0 then
    insert into public.audit_logs (collaboration_id, actor_id, actor_kind, action, from_status, to_status)
    values (c.id, case when v_actor = 'admin' then auth.uid() end, v_actor, 'refund_scheduled',
            'cancelled', 'cancelled');
  end if;
  return v_n;
end;
$$;
revoke execute on function public.admin_cancel_collaboration(uuid, text) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function public.admin_cancel_collaboration(uuid, text) to authenticated, service_role;

-- 8. 列表（只給管理員）
-- 合作案：p_status = active（未結束）｜all｜任一個 collab_status；p_query = 案號（8 碼 id 前綴）或案名、品牌名稱、創作者 handle 的子字串。
create function public.admin_list_collaborations(p_status text, p_query text, p_limit integer, p_offset integer)
returns table (
  id uuid, title text, amount_twd integer, status text, created_at timestamptz, updated_at timestamptz,
  brand_name text, creator_handle text, creator_name text,
  paid boolean, test_paid boolean, refund_pending boolean, total_count bigint
)
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = '' as $$
#variable_conflict use_column
declare
  v_q text := nullif(left(public.clean_text(p_query), 50), '');
  v_like text;
  v_code text;
begin
  perform public.admin_actor();
  if p_status is null or (p_status not in ('active', 'all')
     and p_status not in (select unnest(enum_range(null::public.collab_status))::text)) then
    raise exception 'invalid status' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  v_like := '%' || replace(replace(replace(coalesce(v_q, ''), '\', '\\'), '%', '\%'), '_', '\_') || '%';
  v_code := case when lower(coalesce(v_q, '')) ~ '^[0-9a-f]{8}$' then lower(v_q) end;
  return query
  select c.id, c.title, c.amount_twd, c.status::text, c.created_at, c.updated_at,
         bp.brand_name, cp.handle, p.display_name,
         exists (select 1 from public.payments x where x.collaboration_id = c.id and x.status = 'paid'),
         exists (select 1 from public.payments x where x.collaboration_id = c.id and x.status = 'paid'
                   and x.provider_env = 'stage'),
         exists (select 1 from public.payments x where x.collaboration_id = c.id and x.refund_required),
         count(*) over ()
    from public.collaborations c
    left join public.brand_profiles bp on bp.user_id = c.brand_id
    left join public.creator_profiles cp on cp.user_id = c.creator_id
    left join public.profiles p on p.id = c.creator_id
   where (p_status = 'all'
          or (p_status = 'active' and c.status not in ('completed', 'declined', 'cancelled'))
          or c.status::text = p_status)
     and (v_q is null
          or (v_code is not null and c.id::text like v_code || '%')
          or c.title ilike v_like or bp.brand_name ilike v_like or cp.handle ilike v_like)
   order by c.updated_at desc, c.id
   limit least(greatest(coalesce(p_limit, 50), 1), 100)
  offset greatest(coalesce(p_offset, 0), 0);
end;
$$;
revoke execute on function public.admin_list_collaborations(text, text, integer, integer) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function public.admin_list_collaborations(text, text, integer, integer) to authenticated;

-- 付款單：p_view = all｜pending｜paid｜refund（需退款）；p_collab_id 不是 null 時只列這個合作案。由新到舊。
create function public.admin_list_payments(p_view text, p_limit integer, p_offset integer, p_collab_id uuid default null)
returns table (
  id uuid, collaboration_id uuid, merchant_trade_no text, provider_trade_no text, amount_twd integer,
  status text, payment_method text, provider_env text, paid_at timestamptz, created_at timestamptz,
  refund_required boolean, refunded_at timestamptz, refund_note text, admin_note text,
  collab_title text, collab_status text, brand_name text, creator_handle text, total_count bigint
)
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = '' as $$
#variable_conflict use_column
begin
  perform public.admin_actor();
  if p_view is null or p_view not in ('all', 'pending', 'paid', 'refund') then
    raise exception 'invalid view' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  return query
  select x.id, x.collaboration_id, x.merchant_trade_no, x.provider_trade_no, x.amount_twd,
         x.status, x.payment_method, x.provider_env, x.paid_at, x.created_at,
         x.refund_required, x.refunded_at, x.refund_note, x.admin_note,
         c.title, c.status::text, bp.brand_name, cp.handle, count(*) over ()
    from public.payments x
    join public.collaborations c on c.id = x.collaboration_id
    left join public.brand_profiles bp on bp.user_id = c.brand_id
    left join public.creator_profiles cp on cp.user_id = c.creator_id
   where (p_collab_id is null or x.collaboration_id = p_collab_id)
     and (p_view = 'all'
          or (p_view = 'pending' and x.status = 'pending')
          or (p_view = 'paid' and x.status = 'paid')
          or (p_view = 'refund' and x.refund_required))
   order by x.created_at desc, x.id
   limit least(greatest(coalesce(p_limit, 50), 1), 100)
  offset greatest(coalesce(p_offset, 0), 0);
end;
$$;
revoke execute on function public.admin_list_payments(text, integer, integer, uuid) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function public.admin_list_payments(text, integer, integer, uuid) to authenticated;

-- 待撥款：awaiting_payout 的合作案，依等待時間由舊到新；收款帳戶只回傳銀行代碼與末四碼。
create function public.admin_list_payouts(p_limit integer, p_offset integer)
returns table (
  id uuid, title text, amount_twd integer, updated_at timestamptz, brand_name text,
  creator_id uuid, creator_handle text, creator_name text,
  bank_code text, account_last4 text, account_updated_at timestamptz,
  test_paid boolean, refund_pending boolean, total_count bigint
)
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = '' as $$
#variable_conflict use_column
begin
  perform public.admin_actor();
  return query
  select c.id, c.title, c.amount_twd, c.updated_at, bp.brand_name,
         c.creator_id, cp.handle, p.display_name,
         a.bank_code, a.account_last4, a.updated_at,
         exists (select 1 from public.payments x where x.collaboration_id = c.id and x.status = 'paid'
                   and not x.refund_required and x.provider_env = 'stage'),
         exists (select 1 from public.payments x where x.collaboration_id = c.id and x.refund_required),
         count(*) over ()
    from public.collaborations c
    left join public.brand_profiles bp on bp.user_id = c.brand_id
    left join public.creator_profiles cp on cp.user_id = c.creator_id
    left join public.profiles p on p.id = c.creator_id
    left join public.creator_payout_accounts a on a.user_id = c.creator_id
   where c.status = 'awaiting_payout'
   order by c.updated_at asc, c.id
   limit least(greatest(coalesce(p_limit, 50), 1), 100)
  offset greatest(coalesce(p_offset, 0), 0);
end;
$$;
revoke execute on function public.admin_list_payouts(integer, integer) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function public.admin_list_payouts(integer, integer) to authenticated;

-- 總覽的待辦數字
create function public.admin_overview()
returns table (
  pending_creators bigint, awaiting_payment bigint, awaiting_payout bigint,
  refund_required bigint, open_anomalies bigint, open_flagged_messages bigint
)
language plpgsql stable security definer set search_path = '' as $$
begin
  perform public.admin_actor();
  return query
  select (select count(*) from public.creator_profiles cp where cp.status = 'pending'),
         (select count(*) from public.collaborations c where c.status = 'awaiting_payment'),
         (select count(*) from public.collaborations c where c.status = 'awaiting_payout'),
         (select count(*) from public.payments x where x.refund_required),
         (select count(*) from public.payment_anomalies a where a.resolved_at is null),
         (select count(*) from public.messages m
           where m.flagged_contact
             and not exists (select 1 from public.message_reviews r where r.message_id = m.id));
end;
$$;
revoke execute on function public.admin_overview() from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function public.admin_overview() to authenticated;
```

- [ ] **Step 1: 寫失敗的測試**


`src/lib/admin/query.test.ts`

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  ADMIN_MAX_PAGE, adminHref, offsetOf, pageCount, parseCollabFilter, parseCreatorFilter, parseMessageFilter,
  parseMoneyTab, parsePage, parseQuery,
} from "./query";

describe("parsePage", () => {
  it("正整數照用；其他一律 1；超過上限用上限", () => {
    expect(parsePage("3")).toBe(3);
    expect(parsePage(["2", "9"])).toBe(2);
    for (const v of [undefined, "", "0", "-1", "1.5", "abc", "1e3", " 2"]) expect(parsePage(v)).toBe(1);
    expect(parsePage("99999")).toBe(ADMIN_MAX_PAGE);
    expect(parsePage("99999999")).toBe(1);
  });
});

describe("parseQuery", () => {
  it("去頭尾空白、最多 50 字（emoji 算一個字）", () => {
    expect(parseQuery("  mei ")).toBe("mei");
    expect(parseQuery(undefined)).toBe("");
    expect(parseQuery("😀".repeat(60))).toBe("😀".repeat(50));
  });
});

describe("篩選參數容忍不認得的值", () => {
  it("創作者：預設待審核", () => {
    expect(parseCreatorFilter("rejected")).toBe("rejected");
    expect(parseCreatorFilter("bogus")).toBe("pending");
    expect(parseCreatorFilter(undefined)).toBe("pending");
  });
  it("合作案：預設進行中，接受任一狀態", () => {
    expect(parseCollabFilter("awaiting_payout")).toBe("awaiting_payout");
    expect(parseCollabFilter("all")).toBe("all");
    expect(parseCollabFilter("toString")).toBe("active");
  });
  it("款項與訊息", () => {
    expect(parseMoneyTab("anomalies")).toBe("anomalies");
    expect(parseMoneyTab("x")).toBe("payouts");
    expect(parseMessageFilter("reviewed")).toBe("reviewed");
    expect(parseMessageFilter(["x"])).toBe("open");
  });
});

describe("分頁", () => {
  it("offset 與總頁數", () => {
    expect(offsetOf(1)).toBe(0);
    expect(offsetOf(3)).toBe(100);
    expect(pageCount(0)).toBe(1);
    expect(pageCount(50)).toBe(1);
    expect(pageCount(51)).toBe(2);
  });
});

describe("adminHref", () => {
  it("省略空值、第 1 頁與預設值", () => {
    expect(adminHref("/admin/creators", { status: "pending", q: "", page: 1 }, { status: "pending" })).toBe("/admin/creators");
    expect(adminHref("/admin/creators", { status: "all", q: "mei a", page: 2 }, { status: "pending" }))
      .toBe("/admin/creators?status=all&q=mei+a&page=2");
    expect(adminHref("/admin/payments", { view: "anomalies", state: null })).toBe("/admin/payments?view=anomalies");
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { ADMIN_NOTE_MAX, cleanAdminNote, validateAdminNote } from "./notes";

describe("管理員的原因／說明", () => {
  it("清理：CRLF 轉 LF、去頭尾空白；不是字串回傳 null", () => {
    expect(cleanAdminNote("  已轉帳\r\n序號 1 ")).toBe("已轉帳\n序號 1");
    expect(cleanAdminNote(3)).toBeNull();
  });
  it("必填、最多 500 字、不能有雙向控制字元或 NUL", () => {
    expect(validateAdminNote("", "原因")).toBe("請填寫原因");
    expect(validateAdminNote("a".repeat(ADMIN_NOTE_MAX))).toBeNull();
    expect(validateAdminNote("a".repeat(ADMIN_NOTE_MAX + 1))).toBe("說明最多 500 字");
    expect(validateAdminNote("x‮y")).toBe("說明含有不允許的特殊字元");
    expect(validateAdminNote("x\u0000y")).toBe("說明含有不允許的特殊字元");
  });
});
```

`src/lib/payout-account.test.ts`

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { formatAccountNumber, maskAccount, normalizeDigits, parsePayoutAccountForm, validatePayoutAccount } from "./payout-account";

function form(o: Record<string, string>): FormData {
  const f = new FormData();
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(o)) f.append(k, v);
  return f;
}

describe("收款帳戶", () => {
  it("去掉空白（含全形）與 -", () => {
    expect(normalizeDigits(" 1234-5678　9012 ")).toBe("123456789012");
  });
  it("解析與驗證：與 set_my_payout_account 相同的規則", () => {
    const ok = parsePayoutAccountForm(form({ bankCode: "812", branchCode: "", accountNumber: "1234-5678-9012", accountName: " 王小美 " }));
    expect(ok).toEqual({ bankCode: "812", branchCode: "", accountNumber: "123456789012", accountName: "王小美" });
    expect(validatePayoutAccount(ok)).toBeNull();
    expect(validatePayoutAccount({ bankCode: "81", branchCode: "12", accountNumber: "123456", accountName: "" })).toEqual({
      bankCode: "請填 3 碼銀行代碼（例如 812）",
      branchCode: "分行代碼是 4 碼數字，不知道可以留空",
      accountNumber: "請填 7–16 碼數字的帳號",
      accountName: "請填寫戶名",
    });
    expect(validatePayoutAccount({ ...ok, accountNumber: "1".repeat(17) })).toHaveProperty("accountNumber");
    expect(validatePayoutAccount({ ...ok, accountName: "王‮美" })).toEqual({ accountName: "戶名含有不允許的特殊字元" });
    expect(validatePayoutAccount({ ...ok, accountName: "名".repeat(41) })).toEqual({ accountName: "戶名最多 40 字" });
  });
  it("缺欄位當成空字串", () => {
    expect(parsePayoutAccountForm(form({}))).toEqual({ bankCode: "", branchCode: "", accountNumber: "", accountName: "" });
  });
  it("遮罩與分組", () => {
    expect(maskAccount("9012")).toBe("•••• 9012");
    expect(formatAccountNumber("123456789012")).toBe("1234 5678 9012");
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { isStaleStateMessage } from "@/lib/briefs/errors";
import { COLLAB_STALE_MESSAGE } from "@/lib/collabs/errors";
import { ADMIN_STALE_MESSAGE, adminErrorMessage, paymentResultNotice } from "./errors";

describe("adminErrorMessage", () => {
  it("已知錯誤轉中文；不認得的回通用訊息", () => {
    expect(adminErrorMessage({ message: "test payment cannot be paid out" })).toContain("測試環境");
    expect(adminErrorMessage({ message: "payout account missing" })).toBe("創作者還沒填收款帳戶，請先聯絡創作者");
    expect(adminErrorMessage({ message: "admin is participant" })).toContain("參與者");
    expect(adminErrorMessage({ message: "toString" })).toBe("發生錯誤，請稍後再試");
    expect(adminErrorMessage({})).toBe("發生錯誤，請稍後再試");
  });
  it("狀態已經變了：當成重新整理，不是錯誤", () => {
    expect(adminErrorMessage({ message: "creator status changed" })).toBe(ADMIN_STALE_MESSAGE);
    expect(adminErrorMessage({ message: "collaboration not awaiting payout" })).toBe(COLLAB_STALE_MESSAGE);
    expect(adminErrorMessage({ message: "invalid transition: payout from completed by admin" })).toBe(COLLAB_STALE_MESSAGE);
    expect(isStaleStateMessage(ADMIN_STALE_MESSAGE)).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe("paymentResultNotice", () => {
  it("paid 不另外提示；anomaly 不算成功", () => {
    expect(paymentResultNotice("paid")).toEqual({ ok: true, notice: null });
    expect(paymentResultNotice("already_paid").ok).toBe(true);
    expect(paymentResultNotice("paid_refund_required")).toMatchObject({ ok: true });
    expect(paymentResultNotice("anomaly")).toMatchObject({ ok: false });
    expect(paymentResultNotice(null)).toMatchObject({ ok: false });
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

  payoutBlocker, toAdminCollab, toAdminCreator, toAdminPayment, toAnomaly, toFlaggedMessage, toOverview,
  toPayoutAccountFull, totalOf, type AdminPaymentRow,
} from "./view";

const DAY = 86_400_000;

describe("創作者審核", () => {
  it("每個狀態可以做的動作（與 set_creator_status 相同）", () => {
    expect(creatorActions("pending")).toEqual(["approve", "reject"]);
    expect(creatorActions("rejected")).toEqual(["approve"]);
    expect(creatorActions("approved")).toEqual(["suspend"]);
    expect(creatorActions("suspended")).toEqual(["unsuspend"]);
  });
  it("Row → 顯示用：null 補預設、不認得的狀態當待審核", () => {
    const c = toAdminCreator({
      user_id: "u", handle: null, display_name: null, ig_username: "mei", follower_count: null, region: null,
      categories: null, status: "weird", review_note: null, verification_code: null, created_at: null, total_count: "3",
    });
    expect(c).toMatchObject({ handle: "", followerCount: 0, categories: [], status: "pending" });
    expect(totalOf([{ total_count: "3" }])).toBe(3);
    expect(totalOf([])).toBe(0);
  });
  it("IG 網址會編碼", () => {
    expect(igUrl("mei.daily")).toBe("https://www.instagram.com/mei.daily/");
  });
});

describe("合作案的管理員動作", () => {
  it("等待付款：人工標記；待撥款：撥款；未結束：平台取消", () => {
    expect(adminCollabActions("awaiting_payment")).toEqual({ offlinePayment: true, lostPayment: true, payout: false, cancel: true });
    expect(adminCollabActions("awaiting_payout")).toEqual({ offlinePayment: false, lostPayment: false, payout: true, cancel: true });
    expect(adminCollabActions("completed")).toEqual({ offlinePayment: false, lostPayment: false, payout: false, cancel: false });
    expect(adminCollabActions("cancelled").lostPayment).toBe(true);
  });
  it("列表 Row", () => {
    expect(toAdminCollab({
      id: "c", title: null, amount_twd: null, status: "in_review", created_at: null, updated_at: null, brand_name: null,
      creator_handle: null, creator_name: null, paid: null, test_paid: true, refund_pending: null, total_count: 1,
    })).toMatchObject({ status: "in_review", testPaid: true, refundPending: false, paid: false });
  });
});

const PAY: AdminPaymentRow = {
  id: "p", collaboration_id: "c", merchant_trade_no: "KL2611270000000000AB", provider_trade_no: null, amount_twd: 3000,
  status: "pending", payment_method: "credit", provider_env: null, paid_at: null, created_at: "2026-11-27T00:00:00Z",
  refund_required: false, refunded_at: null, refund_note: null, admin_note: null, collab_title: "x",
  collab_status: "awaiting_payment", brand_name: "b", creator_handle: "mei", total_count: 1,
};

describe("付款單", () => {
  it("來源文字", () => {
    expect(paymentSourceLabel({ merchantTradeNo: "MANABC", method: null })).toBe("人工標記");
    expect(paymentSourceLabel({ merchantTradeNo: "KL1", method: "atm" })).toBe("綠界 ATM");
    expect(paymentSourceLabel({ merchantTradeNo: "KL1", method: "credit" })).toBe("綠界信用卡");
  });
  it("通知遺失可以補登的單：KL 開頭、還沒付款", () => {
    const rows = [
      toAdminPayment(PAY),
      toAdminPayment({ ...PAY, id: "q", status: "paid" }),
      toAdminPayment({ ...PAY, id: "r", merchant_trade_no: "MANX" }),
      toAdminPayment({ ...PAY, id: "s", status: "weird" }),
    ];
    expect(lostPaymentCandidates(rows).map((p) => p.id)).toEqual(["p", "s"]);
    expect(rows[3].status).toBe("pending");
  });
});

describe("撥款", () => {
  it("不能撥款的原因（與 admin_mark_payout 相同順序）", () => {
    expect(payoutBlocker({ testPaid: true, refundPending: true, hasAccount: false })).toContain("測試付款");
    expect(payoutBlocker({ testPaid: false, refundPending: true, hasAccount: false })).toContain("退款");
    expect(payoutBlocker({ testPaid: false, refundPending: false, hasAccount: false })).toBe("創作者還沒填收款帳戶");
    expect(payoutBlocker({ testPaid: false, refundPending: false, hasAccount: true })).toBeNull();
  });
  it("收款帳戶 7 天內改過要提醒", () => {
    const now = Date.parse("2026-11-27T00:00:00Z");
    expect(accountChangedRecently(new Date(now - 6 * DAY).toISOString(), now)).toBe(true);
    expect(accountChangedRecently(new Date(now - 8 * DAY).toISOString(), now)).toBe(false);
    expect(accountChangedRecently(null, now)).toBe(false);
    expect(accountChangedRecently("bad", now)).toBe(false);
  });
  it("完整帳戶：缺必要欄位視為沒有", () => {
    expect(toPayoutAccountFull(undefined)).toBeNull();
    expect(toPayoutAccountFull({ bank_code: "812", branch_code: null, account_number: null, account_name: "x", updated_at: null })).toBeNull();
    expect(toPayoutAccountFull({ bank_code: "812", branch_code: null, account_number: "12345678", account_name: "x", updated_at: null }))
      .toEqual({ bankCode: "812", branchCode: null, accountNumber: "12345678", accountName: "x", updatedAt: "" });
  });
});

describe("其他 Row", () => {
  it("付款異常的類型與來源轉中文；不認得的照原文", () => {
    const a = toAnomaly({
      id: 1, created_at: "t", source: "notify", kind: "amount_mismatch", merchant_trade_no: "KL", provider_trade_no: "",
      expected_amount_twd: 3000, received_amount_twd: 3001, collaboration_id: null, resolved_at: null, resolved_note: null,
    });

    expect(toAnomaly({ ...{ id: 2, created_at: "t", source: "x", kind: "y", merchant_trade_no: "", provider_trade_no: "",
      expected_amount_twd: null, received_amount_twd: null, collaboration_id: null, resolved_at: null, resolved_note: null } }).kind).toBe("y");
  });
  it("違規訊息與總覽", () => {
    expect(toFlaggedMessage({
      id: "m", conversation_id: null, body: null, created_at: null, sender_side: "creator", brand_id: null, brand_name: null,
      creator_id: null, creator_handle: null, creator_name: null, reviewed_at: null, total_count: 1,
    })).toMatchObject({ body: "", senderSide: "creator", reviewedAt: null });
    expect(toOverview(undefined).pendingCreators).toBe(0);
    expect(toOverview({ pending_creators: "2", awaiting_payment: 1, awaiting_payout: null, refund_required: 0,
      open_anomalies: 0, open_flagged_messages: 5 })).toMatchObject({ pendingCreators: 2, awaitingPayout: 0, openFlaggedMessages: 5 });
  });
});
```
