`src/lib/admin/query.ts`

```ts
// 管理後台的網址參數（?status=、?view=、?q=、?page=）。一律容忍不認得的值：退回預設，不報錯。
import type { CollabStatus } from "@/lib/collabs/view";

/** 每頁筆數（資料庫函式的上限是 100）。 */
export const ADMIN_PAGE_SIZE = 50;
/** 頁數上限：避免 ?page=999999999 讓資料庫做很大的 offset。 */
export const ADMIN_MAX_PAGE = 1000;
/** 關鍵字上限（與資料庫 left(…, 50) 相同）。 */
export const ADMIN_QUERY_MAX = 50;

export type SearchParams = Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>;

function first(v: string | string[] | undefined): string | undefined {
  return Array.isArray(v) ? v[0] : v;
}

/** ?page=：1 起算的正整數；其他任何值都是 1，超過上限就是上限。 */
export function parsePage(v: string | string[] | undefined): number {
  const x = first(v);
  if (!x || !/^\d{1,7}$/.test(x)) return 1;
  const n = Number(x);
  return n < 1 ? 1 : Math.min(n, ADMIN_MAX_PAGE);
}

/** ?q=：去掉前後空白、截成 50 字（以 code point 計算）。 */
export function parseQuery(v: string | string[] | undefined): string {
  const x = (first(v) ?? "").trim();
  return Array.from(x).slice(0, ADMIN_QUERY_MAX).join("").trim();
}

function pick<T extends string>(v: string | string[] | undefined, allowed: readonly T[], fallback: T): T {
  const x = first(v);
  return x !== undefined && (allowed as readonly string[]).includes(x) ? (x as T) : fallback;
}

export type CreatorFilter = "pending" | "rejected" | "approved" | "suspended" | "all";
export const CREATOR_FILTERS: { value: CreatorFilter; label: string }[] = [
  { value: "pending", label: "待審核" },
  { value: "rejected", label: "已退回" },
  { value: "approved", label: "已審核" },
  { value: "suspended", label: "已停權" },
  { value: "all", label: "全部" },
];
export function parseCreatorFilter(v: string | string[] | undefined): CreatorFilter {
  return pick(v, CREATOR_FILTERS.map((f) => f.value), "pending");
}

export type CollabFilter = "active" | "all" | CollabStatus;
export const COLLAB_FILTERS: { value: CollabFilter; label: string }[] = [
  { value: "active", label: "進行中" },
  { value: "awaiting_payment", label: "等待付款" },
  { value: "awaiting_submission", label: "待交稿" },
  { value: "in_review", label: "審核中" },
  { value: "awaiting_publish", label: "待發佈" },
  { value: "awaiting_payout", label: "待撥款" },
  { value: "completed", label: "已完成" },
  { value: "cancelled", label: "已取消" },
  { value: "declined", label: "已婉拒" },
  { value: "invited", label: "邀約中" },
  { value: "all", label: "全部" },
];
export function parseCollabFilter(v: string | string[] | undefined): CollabFilter {
  return pick(v, COLLAB_FILTERS.map((f) => f.value), "active");
}

export type MoneyTab = "payouts" | "refunds" | "anomalies" | "payments";
export const MONEY_TABS: { value: MoneyTab; label: string }[] = [
  { value: "payouts", label: "待撥款" },
  { value: "refunds", label: "需退款" },
  { value: "anomalies", label: "付款異常" },
  { value: "payments", label: "全部付款單" },
];
export function parseMoneyTab(v: string | string[] | undefined): MoneyTab {
  return pick(v, MONEY_TABS.map((t) => t.value), "payouts");
}

export type MessageFilter = "open" | "reviewed" | "all";
export const MESSAGE_FILTERS: { value: MessageFilter; label: string }[] = [
  { value: "open", label: "未檢視" },
  { value: "reviewed", label: "已檢視" },
  { value: "all", label: "全部" },
];
export function parseMessageFilter(v: string | string[] | undefined): MessageFilter {
  return pick(v, MESSAGE_FILTERS.map((f) => f.value), "open");
}

/** 第幾筆開始（給資料庫的 p_offset）。 */
export function offsetOf(page: number): number {
  return (page - 1) * ADMIN_PAGE_SIZE;
}

/** 總頁數（至少 1）。 */
export function pageCount(total: number): number {
  return Math.max(1, Math.ceil(Math.max(0, total) / ADMIN_PAGE_SIZE));
}

/**
 * 組管理後台的網址：值是 null、undefined、空字串，或等於該參數的預設值（defaults）時省略，
 * 讓預設狀態的網址保持乾淨（例如 /admin/creators 而不是 /admin/creators?status=pending&page=1）。
 */
export function adminHref(
  path: string,
  params: Record<string, string | number | null | undefined>,
  defaults: Record<string, string | number> = {},
): string {
  const sp = new URLSearchParams();
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(params)) {
    if (v === null || v === undefined || v === "") continue;
    if (k === "page" && v === 1) continue;
    if (Object.hasOwn(defaults, k) && defaults[k] === v) continue;
    sp.set(k, String(v));
  }
  const s = sp.toString();
  return s ? `${path}?${s}` : path;
}
```

`src/lib/admin/notes.ts`

```ts
// 管理員寫的原因／說明（退回、停權、平台取消、轉帳說明、人工付款說明、退款、異常）。
// 規則與 SQL 的 admin_clean_note（20261128000100_admin_core.sql）相同：清理後 1–500 字、沒有雙向控制字元。
// resolve_payment_refund／resolve_payment_anomaly 也是 500 字。
import { hasBadChar } from "@/lib/validation/brief";
import { cleanLongText } from "@/lib/validation/collab";

export const ADMIN_NOTE_MAX = 500;

/** 清理（CRLF → LF、去頭尾空白）；不是字串回傳 null。 */
export function cleanAdminNote(v: unknown): string | null {
  return typeof v === "string" ? cleanLongText(v) : null;
}

/** 錯誤訊息或 null（通過）。 */
export function validateAdminNote(note: string, label = "說明"): string | null {
  if (note.length === 0) return `請填寫${label}`;
  if (hasBadChar(note)) return `${label}含有不允許的特殊字元`;
  if ([...note].length > ADMIN_NOTE_MAX) return `${label}最多 ${ADMIN_NOTE_MAX} 字`;
  return null;
}
```

`src/lib/payout-account.ts`

```ts
// 創作者的收款帳戶。規則與 SQL 的 set_my_payout_account（20261128000200_admin_money.sql）相同，改一邊要改另一邊：
// 銀行代碼 3 碼、分行代碼 4 碼（選填）、帳號 7–16 碼數字（空白與「-」會去掉）、戶名 1–40 字。
import { hasBadChar } from "@/lib/validation/brief";
import { cleanText } from "@/lib/validation/onboarding";

export const BANK_CODE_RE = /^[0-9]{3}$/;
export const BRANCH_CODE_RE = /^[0-9]{4}$/;
export const ACCOUNT_NUMBER_RE = /^[0-9]{7,16}$/;
export const ACCOUNT_NAME_MAX = 40;

export type PayoutAccountInput = { bankCode: string; branchCode: string; accountNumber: string; accountName: string };

/** 去掉空白（含全形空白）與「-」。 */
export function normalizeDigits(v: string): string {
  return v.replace(/[\s　-]/g, "");
}

function str(fd: FormData, key: string): string {
  const v = fd.get(key);
  return typeof v === "string" ? v.slice(0, 200) : "";
}

export function parsePayoutAccountForm(fd: FormData): PayoutAccountInput {
  return {
    bankCode: normalizeDigits(str(fd, "bankCode")),
    branchCode: normalizeDigits(str(fd, "branchCode")),
    accountNumber: normalizeDigits(str(fd, "accountNumber")),
    accountName: cleanText(str(fd, "accountName")),
  };
}

export function validatePayoutAccount(i: PayoutAccountInput): Record<string, string> | null {
  const e: Record<string, string> = {};
  if (!BANK_CODE_RE.test(i.bankCode)) e.bankCode = "請填 3 碼銀行代碼（例如 812）";
  if (i.branchCode !== "" && !BRANCH_CODE_RE.test(i.branchCode)) e.branchCode = "分行代碼是 4 碼數字，不知道可以留空";
  if (!ACCOUNT_NUMBER_RE.test(i.accountNumber)) e.accountNumber = "請填 7–16 碼數字的帳號";
  if (i.accountName === "") e.accountName = "請填寫戶名";
  else if (hasBadChar(i.accountName)) e.accountName = "戶名含有不允許的特殊字元";
  else if ([...i.accountName].length > ACCOUNT_NAME_MAX) e.accountName = `戶名最多 ${ACCOUNT_NAME_MAX} 字`;
  return Object.keys(e).length > 0 ? e : null;
}

/** 列表與本人畫面只顯示末四碼。 */
export function maskAccount(last4: string): string {
  return `•••• ${last4}`;
}

/** 撥款畫面的完整帳號：每 4 碼空一格，方便對照轉帳。 */
export function formatAccountNumber(v: string): string {
  return v.replace(/(\d{4})(?=\d)/g, "$1 ");
}
```

`src/lib/admin/errors.ts`

```ts
// 管理後台 definer 函式（20261128000100、000200）與 resolve_payment_*（計畫 8）的錯誤 → 畫面文字。
import { COLLAB_STALE_MESSAGE } from "@/lib/collabs/errors";

type DbError = { code?: string; message?: string };

/** 資料已經被處理過（連點、另一位管理員、另一個分頁）：畫面重新整理即可，不當成錯誤。 */
export const ADMIN_STALE_MESSAGE = "資料已經更新";

const MESSAGES: Record<string, string> = {
  "not authenticated": "登入已逾時，請重新登入",
  "not admin": "這個帳號不是管理員",
  "admin is participant": "你是這個合作案的參與者，不能用管理員身分處理",
  "invalid note": "說明必填，最多 500 字，不能有特殊字元",
  "invalid reason": "原因必填，最多 500 字，不能有特殊字元",
  "creator not found": "找不到這位創作者",
  "creator status changed": ADMIN_STALE_MESSAGE,
  "message not found": "找不到這則訊息",
  "message not flagged": "這則訊息沒有被標記",
  "collaboration not found": "找不到這個合作案",
  "collaboration not awaiting payment": COLLAB_STALE_MESSAGE,
  "collaboration not awaiting payout": COLLAB_STALE_MESSAGE,
  "collaboration already closed": COLLAB_STALE_MESSAGE,
  "test payment cannot be paid out": "這筆是綠界測試環境的付款（沒有實際收款），不能撥款",
  "no paid payment": "這個合作案沒有已付款的款項，不能撥款",
  "refund pending": "這個合作案有待退款的款項，請先處理退款",
  "payout account missing": "創作者還沒填收款帳戶，請先聯絡創作者",
  "invalid env": "綠界環境未設定，無法確認這張付款單；請用 README 的備用 SQL",
  "invalid provider_trade_no": "綠界交易編號是 1–20 位英數字",
  "invalid amount": "請填綠界後台顯示的金額",
  "payment not found": "找不到這張付款單",
  "not an online payment": "只有 KL 開頭的綠界付款單可以用這個方式",
  "refund not required": ADMIN_STALE_MESSAGE,
  "anomaly not found": "找不到這筆付款異常",
  "anomaly already resolved": ADMIN_STALE_MESSAGE,
};

export function adminErrorMessage(error: DbError): string {
  const m = error.message ?? "";
  // transition_collaboration：invalid transition: <動作> from <狀態> by <身分>
  if (m.startsWith("invalid transition")) return COLLAB_STALE_MESSAGE;
  return Object.hasOwn(MESSAGES, m) ? MESSAGES[m] : "發生錯誤，請稍後再試";
}

/** record_payment_success 的結果 → 給管理員的說明。paid 是正常結果（null = 不用另外提示）。 */
export function paymentResultNotice(result: unknown): { ok: boolean; notice: string | null } {
  switch (result) {
    case "paid":
      return { ok: true, notice: null };
    case "already_paid":
      return { ok: true, notice: "這張付款單本來就是已付款，沒有改變任何狀態" };
    case "paid_refund_required":
      return { ok: true, notice: "已記錄付款，但合作案已經不在等待付款：這筆款項標記為需退款" };
    case "anomaly":
      return { ok: false, notice: "金額與付款單不符，已記錄為付款異常（款項 → 付款異常）；合作案狀態沒有改變" };
    default:
      return { ok: false, notice: "發生錯誤，請稍後再試" };
  }
}
```

`src/lib/admin/view.ts`

```ts
// 管理後台：資料庫 Row → 顯示用物件、狀態文字、可以做哪些動作。
// 產生的型別把 RETURNS TABLE 的欄位都標成非 null，實際上可能是 null，所以 Row 型別自己定義。
import { toCollabStatus, type CollabStatus } from "@/lib/collabs/view";

const DAY_MS = 86_400_000;

// ── 創作者審核 ──

export type AdminCreatorStatus = "pending" | "approved" | "suspended" | "rejected";
export const CREATOR_STATUS_LABEL: Record<AdminCreatorStatus, string> = {
  pending: "待審核",
  approved: "已審核",
  suspended: "已停權",
  rejected: "已退回",
};

function toCreatorStatus(v: unknown): AdminCreatorStatus {
  return v === "approved" || v === "suspended" || v === "rejected" ? v : "pending";
}

export type AdminCreatorRow = {
  user_id: string;
  handle: string | null;
  display_name: string | null;
  ig_username: string | null;
  follower_count: number | null;
  region: string | null;
  categories: string[] | null;
  status: string | null;
  review_note: string | null;
  verification_code: string | null;
  created_at: string | null;
  total_count: number | string | null;
};

export type AdminCreator = {
  userId: string;
  handle: string;
  displayName: string;
  igUsername: string;
  followerCount: number;
  region: string | null;
  categories: string[];
  status: AdminCreatorStatus;
  reviewNote: string | null;
  verificationCode: string | null;
  createdAt: string;
};

export function toAdminCreator(r: AdminCreatorRow): AdminCreator {
  return {
    userId: r.user_id,
    handle: r.handle ?? "",
    displayName: r.display_name ?? "",
    igUsername: r.ig_username ?? "",
    followerCount: r.follower_count ?? 0,
    region: r.region ?? null,
    categories: r.categories ?? [],
    status: toCreatorStatus(r.status),
    reviewNote: r.review_note ?? null,
    verificationCode: r.verification_code ?? null,
    createdAt: r.created_at ?? "",
  };
}

export type CreatorReviewAction = "approve" | "reject" | "suspend" | "unsuspend";

/** 每個狀態可以做的動作（與 set_creator_status 的規則相同）。 */
export function creatorActions(s: AdminCreatorStatus): CreatorReviewAction[] {
  switch (s) {
    case "pending":
      return ["approve", "reject"];
    case "rejected":
      return ["approve"];
    case "approved":
      return ["suspend"];
    case "suspended":
      return ["unsuspend"];
  }
}

/** IG 個人頁（審核時打開確認驗證碼）。 */
export function igUrl(username: string): string {
  return `https://www.instagram.com/${encodeURIComponent(username)}/`;
}

// ── 列表共用 ──

/** total_count 在每一列都一樣（window 函式）；PostgREST 把 bigint 轉成數字或字串，兩種都接受。 */
export function totalOf(rows: { total_count: number | string | null }[]): number {
  const n = Number(rows[0]?.total_count ?? 0);
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
}

// ── 合作案 ──

export type AdminCollabRow = {
  id: string;
  title: string | null;
  amount_twd: number | null;
  status: string | null;
  created_at: string | null;
  updated_at: string | null;
  brand_name: string | null;
  creator_handle: string | null;
  creator_name: string | null;
  paid: boolean | null;
  test_paid: boolean | null;
  refund_pending: boolean | null;
  total_count: number | string | null;
};

export type AdminCollab = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  amount: number;
  status: CollabStatus;
  updatedAt: string;
  brandName: string;
  creatorHandle: string;
  creatorName: string;
  paid: boolean;
  testPaid: boolean;
  refundPending: boolean;
};

export function toAdminCollab(r: AdminCollabRow): AdminCollab {
  return {
    id: r.id,
    title: r.title ?? "",
    amount: r.amount_twd ?? 0,
    status: toCollabStatus(r.status),
    updatedAt: r.updated_at ?? "",
    brandName: r.brand_name ?? "",
    creatorHandle: r.creator_handle ?? "",
    creatorName: r.creator_name ?? "",
    paid: r.paid === true,
    testPaid: r.test_paid === true,
    refundPending: r.refund_pending === true,
  };
}

const CLOSED: readonly CollabStatus[] = ["completed", "declined", "cancelled"];

export type AdminCollabActions = { offlinePayment: boolean; lostPayment: boolean; payout: boolean; cancel: boolean };

/** 合作案詳情的管理員動作：等待付款 → 人工標記已付款；待撥款 → 撥款；未結束 → 平台取消。 */
export function adminCollabActions(s: CollabStatus): AdminCollabActions {
  return {
    offlinePayment: s === "awaiting_payment",
    lostPayment: s === "awaiting_payment" || s === "cancelled",
    payout: s === "awaiting_payout",
    cancel: !CLOSED.includes(s),
  };
}

// ── 付款單 ──

export type AdminPaymentRow = {
  id: string;
  collaboration_id: string;
  merchant_trade_no: string | null;
  provider_trade_no: string | null;
  amount_twd: number | null;
  status: string | null;
  payment_method: string | null;
  provider_env: string | null;
  paid_at: string | null;
  created_at: string | null;
  refund_required: boolean | null;
  refunded_at: string | null;
  refund_note: string | null;
  admin_note: string | null;
  collab_title: string | null;
  collab_status: string | null;
  brand_name: string | null;
  creator_handle: string | null;
  total_count: number | string | null;
};

export type AdminPayment = {
  id: string;
  collabId: string;
  merchantTradeNo: string;
  providerTradeNo: string | null;
  amount: number;
  status: "pending" | "paid" | "failed";
  method: string | null;
  env: string | null;
  paidAt: string | null;
  createdAt: string;
  refundRequired: boolean;
  refundedAt: string | null;
  refundNote: string | null;
  adminNote: string | null;
  collabTitle: string;
  collabStatus: CollabStatus;
  brandName: string;
  creatorHandle: string;
};

export function toAdminPayment(r: AdminPaymentRow): AdminPayment {
  return {
    id: r.id,
    collabId: r.collaboration_id,
    merchantTradeNo: r.merchant_trade_no ?? "",
    providerTradeNo: r.provider_trade_no ?? null,
    amount: r.amount_twd ?? 0,
    status: r.status === "paid" || r.status === "failed" ? r.status : "pending",
    method: r.payment_method ?? null,
    env: r.provider_env ?? null,
    paidAt: r.paid_at ?? null,
    createdAt: r.created_at ?? "",
    refundRequired: r.refund_required === true,
    refundedAt: r.refunded_at ?? null,
    refundNote: r.refund_note ?? null,
    adminNote: r.admin_note ?? null,
    collabTitle: r.collab_title ?? "",
    collabStatus: toCollabStatus(r.collab_status),
    brandName: r.brand_name ?? "",
    creatorHandle: r.creator_handle ?? "",
  };
}

export const PAYMENT_STATUS_LABEL: Record<AdminPayment["status"], string> = {
  pending: "未付款",
  paid: "已付款",
  failed: "失敗",
};

/** 付款單的來源：MAN = 人工（線下）；其他是綠界（信用卡／ATM）。 */
export function paymentSourceLabel(p: Pick<AdminPayment, "merchantTradeNo" | "method">): string {
  if (p.merchantTradeNo.startsWith("MAN")) return "人工標記";
  if (p.method === "credit") return "綠界信用卡";
  if (p.method === "atm") return "綠界 ATM";
  return "綠界";
}

/** 「綠界已扣款、但沒收到通知」可以處理的單：KL 開頭、還沒付款。 */
export function lostPaymentCandidates(payments: AdminPayment[]): AdminPayment[] {
  return payments.filter((p) => p.merchantTradeNo.startsWith("KL") && p.status !== "paid");
}

export type AdminPayoutRow = {

  amount_twd: number | null;
  updated_at: string | null;

  account_updated_at: string | null;
  test_paid: boolean | null;
  refund_pending: boolean | null;
  total_count: number | string | null;
};

export type AdminPayout = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  amount: number;
  waitingSince: string;
  brandName: string;
  creatorHandle: string;
  creatorName: string;
  bankCode: string | null;
  accountLast4: string | null;
  accountUpdatedAt: string | null;
  testPaid: boolean;
  refundPending: boolean;
};

export function toAdminPayout(r: AdminPayoutRow): AdminPayout {
  return {
    id: r.id,
    title: r.title ?? "",
    amount: r.amount_twd ?? 0,
    waitingSince: r.updated_at ?? "",
    brandName: r.brand_name ?? "",
    creatorHandle: r.creator_handle ?? "",
    creatorName: r.creator_name ?? "",
    bankCode: r.bank_code ?? null,
    accountLast4: r.account_last4 ?? null,
    accountUpdatedAt: r.account_updated_at ?? null,
    testPaid: r.test_paid === true,
    refundPending: r.refund_pending === true,
  };
}

/** 不能撥款的原因（與 admin_mark_payout 的檢查相同）；可以撥款回傳 null。 */
export function payoutBlocker(p: { testPaid: boolean; refundPending: boolean; hasAccount: boolean }): string | null {
  if (p.testPaid) return "測試付款（未實際收款），不能撥款";
  if (p.refundPending) return "有待退款的款項，請先處理退款";
  if (!p.hasAccount) return "創作者還沒填收款帳戶";
  return null;
}

/** 收款帳戶 7 天內改過：撥款前提醒管理員跟創作者確認（帳號被盜改帳戶的風險）。 */
export function accountChangedRecently(updatedAt: string | null, nowMs: number): boolean {
  if (!updatedAt) return false;
  const t = Date.parse(updatedAt);
  return !Number.isNaN(t) && nowMs - t < 7 * DAY_MS;
}

export type PayoutAccountFull = {
  bankCode: string;
  branchCode: string | null;
  accountNumber: string;
  accountName: string;
  updatedAt: string;
};

export type PayoutAccountRow = {
  bank_code: string | null;
  branch_code: string | null;
  account_number: string | null;
  account_name: string | null;
  updated_at: string | null;
};

export function toPayoutAccountFull(r: PayoutAccountRow | undefined): PayoutAccountFull | null {
  if (!r?.bank_code || !r.account_number || !r.account_name) return null;
  return {
    bankCode: r.bank_code,
    branchCode: r.branch_code ?? null,
    accountNumber: r.account_number,
    accountName: r.account_name,
    updatedAt: r.updated_at ?? "",
  };
}

export const ANOMALY_KIND_LABEL: Record<string, string> = {

  price_mismatch: "付款單與報價不符",
};

  notify: "付款通知",
  payment_info: "ATM 取號通知",
};

export type AnomalyRow = {
  id: number;
  created_at: string;
  source: string;
  kind: string;
  merchant_trade_no: string;
  provider_trade_no: string;
  expected_amount_twd: number | null;
  received_amount_twd: number | null;
  collaboration_id: string | null;
  resolved_at: string | null;
  resolved_note: string | null;
};

export type Anomaly = {
  id: number;
  createdAt: string;
  source: string;
  kind: string;
  merchantTradeNo: string;
  providerTradeNo: string;
  expected: number | null;
  received: number | null;
  collabId: string | null;
  resolvedAt: string | null;
  resolvedNote: string | null;
};

export function toAnomaly(r: AnomalyRow): Anomaly {
  return {
    id: r.id,
    createdAt: r.created_at,
    source: ANOMALY_SOURCE_LABEL[r.source] ?? r.source,
    kind: ANOMALY_KIND_LABEL[r.kind] ?? r.kind,
    merchantTradeNo: r.merchant_trade_no,
    providerTradeNo: r.provider_trade_no,
    expected: r.expected_amount_twd,
    received: r.received_amount_twd,
    collabId: r.collaboration_id,
    resolvedAt: r.resolved_at,
    resolvedNote: r.resolved_note,
  };
}

// ── 違規訊息 ──

export type FlaggedMessageRow = {
  id: string;
  conversation_id: string | null;
  body: string | null;
  created_at: string | null;
  sender_side: string | null;
  brand_id: string | null;
  brand_name: string | null;
  creator_id: string | null;
  creator_handle: string | null;
  creator_name: string | null;
  reviewed_at: string | null;
  total_count: number | string | null;
};

  id: string;
  body: string;
  createdAt: string;
  senderSide: "brand" | "creator";
  brandName: string;
  creatorId: string | null;
  creatorHandle: string;
  creatorName: string;
  reviewedAt: string | null;
};

export function toFlaggedMessage(r: FlaggedMessageRow): FlaggedMessage {
  return {
    id: r.id,
    body: r.body ?? "",
    createdAt: r.created_at ?? "",
    senderSide: r.sender_side === "brand" ? "brand" : "creator",
    brandName: r.brand_name ?? "",
    creatorId: r.creator_id ?? null,
    creatorHandle: r.creator_handle ?? "",
    creatorName: r.creator_name ?? "",

  };

// ── 總覽 ──

export type Overview = {
  pendingCreators: number;
  awaitingPayment: number;
  awaitingPayout: number;
  refundRequired: number;
  openAnomalies: number;
  openFlaggedMessages: number;
};

export type OverviewRow = {
  pending_creators: number | string | null;
  awaiting_payment: number | string | null;
  awaiting_payout: number | string | null;
  refund_required: number | string | null;
  open_anomalies: number | string | null;
  open_flagged_messages: number | string | null;
};

function n(v: number | string | null | undefined): number {
  const x = Number(v ?? 0);
  return Number.isFinite(x) ? x : 0;
}

export function toOverview(r: OverviewRow | undefined): Overview {
  return {
    pendingCreators: n(r?.pending_creators),
    awaitingPayment: n(r?.awaiting_payment),
    awaitingPayout: n(r?.awaiting_payout),
    refundRequired: n(r?.refund_required),
    openAnomalies: n(r?.open_anomalies),
    openFlaggedMessages: n(r?.open_flagged_messages),
  };
}
```

```ts
describe("管理員（計畫 9）", () => {
  it("沒有 profile 的管理員 → /admin（不進填資料頁）", () => {
    expect(resolvePostLoginPath({ role: null, requestedRole: "brand", next: null, isAdmin: true })).toBe("/admin");
  });
  it("next 在 /admin 底下才採用", () => {
    expect(resolvePostLoginPath({ role: null, requestedRole: null, next: "/admin/payments?view=refunds", isAdmin: true }))
      .toBe("/admin/payments?view=refunds");
    expect(resolvePostLoginPath({ role: "brand", requestedRole: null, next: "/admin", isAdmin: true })).toBe("/admin");
    expect(resolvePostLoginPath({ role: "brand", requestedRole: null, next: "/adminx", isAdmin: true })).toBe("/brand");
    expect(resolvePostLoginPath({ role: null, requestedRole: null, next: "//evil.com/admin", isAdmin: true })).toBe("/admin");
  });
  it("不是管理員：/admin 的 next 不採用", () => {
    expect(resolvePostLoginPath({ role: "brand", requestedRole: null, next: "/admin", isAdmin: false })).toBe("/brand");
    expect(resolvePostLoginPath({ role: null, requestedRole: null, next: "/admin" })).toBe("/onboarding");
  });
});
```

`src/lib/auth/post-login.ts`：

找到：

```ts
/** 登入後要去哪裡。規則見 docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-23-auth-design.md 第 1 節；/c/<handle> 見計畫 3。 */
export function resolvePostLoginPath(a: { role: Role | null; requestedRole: string | null; next: string | null }): string {
  if (!a.role) return isRole(a.requestedRole) ? `/onboarding/${a.requestedRole}` : "/onboarding";
  const home = homeFor(a.role);
  const next = safeNextPath(a.next);
```

換成：

```ts
/**
 * 登入後要去哪裡。規則見 docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-23-auth-design.md 第 1 節；/c/<handle> 見計畫 3。
 * 管理員（計畫 9）：next 在 /admin 底下就採用；沒有 profile 的管理員帳號一律到 /admin（不進填資料頁）。
 */
export function resolvePostLoginPath(a: {
  role: Role | null; requestedRole: string | null; next: string | null; isAdmin?: boolean;
}): string {
  const next = safeNextPath(a.next);
  if (a.isAdmin && next && (next === "/admin" || next.startsWith("/admin/") || next.startsWith("/admin?"))) return next;
  if (!a.role) {
    if (a.isAdmin) return "/admin";
    return isRole(a.requestedRole) ? `/onboarding/${a.requestedRole}` : "/onboarding";
  }
  const home = homeFor(a.role);
```

```ts
    supabase.rpc("is_admin"),
  ]);
  if (error || adminErr) return "/login?error=profile";
  return resolvePostLoginPath({ role: profile?.role ?? null, requestedRole, next, isAdmin: isAdmin === true });
```

```ts
/**

```

換成：

```ts
/** 目前使用者是不是管理員（admins 表；is_admin() 只授權給 authenticated）。沒有登入是 false。會讀 cookie。 */
export const getIsAdmin = cache(async (): Promise<boolean> => {
  const me = await getCurrentUser();
  if (!me) return false;
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("is_admin");
  if (error) throw error;
  return data === true;
});

/**
 * 管理後台的守門（計畫 9）：每個 /admin 頁面與 Server Action 都要呼叫（同 requireRole 的理由）。
 * 未登入導到登入頁；不是管理員導回自己的首頁（沒有 profile 的導到 /onboarding）。
 * 管理員不一定有 profile：通常是專用帳號，只在 admins 表裡。
 */
export async function requireAdmin(): Promise<CurrentUser> {
  const me = await getCurrentUser();
  if (!me) redirect("/login?next=/admin");
  if (!(await getIsAdmin())) redirect(me.profile ? homeFor(me.profile.role) : "/onboarding");
  return me;
}

/**
 * 頁面與 Server Action 的角色守門：
```

`src/app/admin/actions.test.ts`

```ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

const { requireAdmin, rpc, revalidatePath, readEcpayConfig } = vi.hoisted(() => ({
  requireAdmin: vi.fn(), rpc: vi.fn(), revalidatePath: vi.fn(), readEcpayConfig: vi.fn(),
}));
vi.mock("server-only", () => ({}));
vi.mock("next/cache", () => ({ revalidatePath }));
vi.mock("@/lib/auth/current-user", () => ({ requireAdmin }));
vi.mock("@/lib/supabase/server", () => ({ createClient: async () => ({ rpc }) }));
vi.mock("@/lib/payments/config", () => ({ readEcpayConfig }));

import {
  adminCancelAction, markMessageReviewedAction, markPayoutAction, recordLostPaymentAction, recordOfflinePaymentAction,
  resolveAnomalyAction, resolveRefundAction, setCreatorStatusAction,
} from "./actions";

const UID = "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000a11";
const COLLAB = "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000ac1";
const BAD = { ok: false, error: "資料格式不正確，請重新整理頁面" };

beforeEach(() => {
  requireAdmin.mockReset().mockResolvedValue({ id: "admin-1" });
  rpc.mockReset().mockResolvedValue({ data: null, error: null });
  revalidatePath.mockReset();
  readEcpayConfig.mockReset().mockReturnValue({ env: "stage" });
});

describe("setCreatorStatusAction", () => {
  it("先檢查管理員；參數不對不碰資料庫", async () => {
    expect(await setCreatorStatusAction("x", "approve")).toEqual(BAD);
    expect(await setCreatorStatusAction(UID, "delete")).toEqual(BAD);
    expect(requireAdmin).toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(rpc).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  it("退回與停權要寫原因", async () => {
    expect(await setCreatorStatusAction(UID, "reject", "  ")).toEqual({ ok: false, error: "請填寫原因" });
    expect(await setCreatorStatusAction(UID, "suspend", 3)).toEqual(BAD);
    expect(rpc).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  it("核准不帶原因；退回帶清理過的原因", async () => {
    expect(await setCreatorStatusAction(UID, "approve")).toEqual({ ok: true });
    expect(rpc).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith("set_creator_status", { p_user_id: UID, p_action: "approve" });
    await setCreatorStatusAction(UID, "reject", " 找不到驗證碼 ");
    expect(rpc).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith("set_creator_status", { p_user_id: UID, p_action: "reject", p_reason: "找不到驗證碼" });
    expect(revalidatePath).toHaveBeenCalledWith("/admin", "layout");
  });
  it("資料庫錯誤轉中文", async () => {
    rpc.mockResolvedValue({ data: null, error: { code: "55000", message: "creator status changed" } });
    expect(await setCreatorStatusAction(UID, "approve")).toEqual({ ok: false, error: "資料已經更新" });
  });
});

describe("markMessageReviewedAction", () => {
  it("id 不對不碰資料庫；成功", async () => {
    expect(await markMessageReviewedAction(1)).toEqual(BAD);
    expect(await markMessageReviewedAction(UID)).toEqual({ ok: true });
    expect(rpc).toHaveBeenCalledWith("mark_message_reviewed", { p_message_id: UID });
  });
});

describe("recordOfflinePaymentAction", () => {
  it("說明必填", async () => {
    expect(await recordOfflinePaymentAction(COLLAB, "")).toEqual({ ok: false, error: "請填寫說明" });
    expect(rpc).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  it("paid：成功、通知雙方的頁面重新整理", async () => {
    rpc.mockResolvedValue({ data: "paid", error: null });
    expect(await recordOfflinePaymentAction(COLLAB, "匯款末五碼 12345")).toEqual({ ok: true });
    expect(rpc).toHaveBeenCalledWith("admin_record_offline_payment", { p_collab_id: COLLAB, p_note: "匯款末五碼 12345" });
    expect(revalidatePath).toHaveBeenCalledWith(`/creator/collabs/${COLLAB}`);
  });
  it("狀態已變：回傳重新整理訊息", async () => {
    rpc.mockResolvedValue({ data: null, error: { code: "55000", message: "collaboration not awaiting payment" } });
    expect(await recordOfflinePaymentAction(COLLAB, "x")).toEqual({ ok: false, error: "合作案的狀態已經更新" });
  });
});

describe("recordLostPaymentAction", () => {
  const args = [COLLAB, "KL2611270123456789AB", " 2411270000000001 ", "3,000", "綠界後台確認"] as const;
  it("格式檢查", async () => {
    expect(await recordLostPaymentAction(COLLAB, "MAN123", "1", "1", "x")).toEqual(BAD);
    expect(await recordLostPaymentAction(COLLAB, "KL1", "有空 白", "1", "x")).toEqual({ ok: false, error: "綠界交易編號是 1–20 位英數字" });
    expect(await recordLostPaymentAction(COLLAB, "KL1", "1", "abc", "x")).toEqual({ ok: false, error: "請填綠界後台顯示的金額（整數）" });
    expect(rpc).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  it("環境用這個部署的 ECPAY_ENV；未設定時拒絕", async () => {
    readEcpayConfig.mockReturnValue(null);
    expect(await recordLostPaymentAction(...args)).toMatchObject({ ok: false });
    expect(rpc).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    readEcpayConfig.mockReturnValue({ env: "prod" });
    rpc.mockResolvedValue({ data: "paid", error: null });
    expect(await recordLostPaymentAction(...args)).toEqual({ ok: true });
    expect(rpc).toHaveBeenCalledWith("admin_record_lost_payment", {
      p_merchant_trade_no: "KL2611270123456789AB", p_provider_trade_no: "2411270000000001", p_amount_twd: 3000,
      p_env: "prod", p_note: "綠界後台確認",
    });
  });
  it("金額不符：記錄為異常，回報失敗", async () => {
    rpc.mockResolvedValue({ data: "anomaly", error: null });
    const res = await recordLostPaymentAction(...args);
    expect(res.ok).toBe(false);
    rpc.mockResolvedValue({ data: "paid_refund_required", error: null });
    expect(await recordLostPaymentAction(...args)).toMatchObject({ ok: true, notice: expect.stringContaining("需退款") });
  });
});

describe("撥款、平台取消、退款、異常", () => {
  it("撥款：轉帳說明必填；測試付款被拒", async () => {
    expect(await markPayoutAction(COLLAB, " ")).toEqual({ ok: false, error: "請填寫轉帳說明" });
    rpc.mockResolvedValue({ data: null, error: { code: "55000", message: "test payment cannot be paid out" } });
    expect(await markPayoutAction(COLLAB, "已轉帳")).toMatchObject({ ok: false, error: expect.stringContaining("測試環境") });
    rpc.mockResolvedValue({ data: null, error: null });
    expect(await markPayoutAction(COLLAB, "已轉帳")).toEqual({ ok: true });
    expect(rpc).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith("admin_mark_payout", { p_collab_id: COLLAB, p_note: "已轉帳" });
  });
  it("平台取消：有已付款的款項時提示需退款", async () => {
    rpc.mockResolvedValue({ data: 1, error: null });
    expect(await adminCancelAction(COLLAB, "雙方同意")).toMatchObject({ ok: true, notice: expect.stringContaining("1 筆") });
    rpc.mockResolvedValue({ data: 0, error: null });
    expect(await adminCancelAction(COLLAB, "雙方同意")).toEqual({ ok: true });
    expect(await adminCancelAction(COLLAB, "")).toEqual({ ok: false, error: "請填寫原因" });
  });
  it("退款與異常", async () => {
    expect(await resolveRefundAction("x", "已刷退")).toEqual(BAD);
    expect(await resolveRefundAction(COLLAB, "已刷退")).toEqual({ ok: true });
    expect(rpc).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith("resolve_payment_refund", { p_payment_id: COLLAB, p_note: "已刷退" });
    expect(await resolveAnomalyAction("1", "x")).toEqual(BAD);
    expect(await resolveAnomalyAction(1.5, "x")).toEqual(BAD);
    expect(await resolveAnomalyAction(7, "已確認")).toEqual({ ok: true });
    expect(rpc).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith("resolve_payment_anomaly", { p_id: 7, p_note: "已確認" });
  });
});
```

```ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

const { requireRole, rpc, revalidatePath } = vi.hoisted(() => ({ requireRole: vi.fn(), rpc: vi.fn(), revalidatePath: vi.fn() }));
vi.mock("server-only", () => ({}));
vi.mock("next/cache", () => ({ revalidatePath }));
vi.mock("@/lib/auth/current-user", () => ({ requireRole }));
vi.mock("@/lib/supabase/server", () => ({ createClient: async () => ({ rpc }) }));

import { requestReviewAction } from "../actions";
import { savePayoutAccountAction } from "./actions";

function form(o: Record<string, string>): FormData {
  const f = new FormData();
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(o)) f.append(k, v);
  return f;
}

beforeEach(() => {
  requireRole.mockReset().mockResolvedValue({ id: "creator-1" });
  rpc.mockReset().mockResolvedValue({ data: null, error: null });
  revalidatePath.mockReset();
});

describe("savePayoutAccountAction", () => {
  it("只給創作者；格式錯誤不碰資料庫", async () => {
    expect(await savePayoutAccountAction({}, form({ bankCode: "8", accountNumber: "1", accountName: "x" })))
      .toMatchObject({ fieldErrors: { bankCode: expect.any(String), accountNumber: expect.any(String) } });
    expect(requireRole).toHaveBeenCalledWith("creator");
    expect(rpc).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  it("成功：送清理過的值", async () => {
    expect(await savePayoutAccountAction({}, form({ bankCode: "812", branchCode: "", accountNumber: "1234-5678 9012", accountName: " 王小美 " })))
      .toEqual({ ok: true });
    expect(rpc).toHaveBeenCalledWith("set_my_payout_account", {
      p_bank_code: "812", p_branch_code: "", p_account_number: "123456789012", p_account_name: "王小美",
    });
  });
  it("資料庫的欄位錯誤對應到欄位", async () => {
    rpc.mockResolvedValue({ data: null, error: { code: "22023", message: "invalid account_name" } });
    expect(await savePayoutAccountAction({}, form({ bankCode: "812", accountNumber: "12345678", accountName: "x" })))
      .toEqual({ fieldErrors: { accountName: "格式不正確" } });
  });
});

describe("requestReviewAction", () => {
  it("成功；狀態已變時提示重新整理", async () => {
    expect(await requestReviewAction()).toEqual({ ok: true });
    expect(rpc).toHaveBeenCalledWith("request_creator_review");
    rpc.mockResolvedValue({ data: null, error: { code: "55000", message: "creator status changed" } });
    expect(await requestReviewAction()).toEqual({ ok: false, error: "狀態已經更新，請重新整理" });
  });
});
```

`src/lib/admin/load.ts`

```ts
import "server-only";
import { createClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import { ADMIN_PAGE_SIZE, offsetOf, type CollabFilter, type CreatorFilter, type MessageFilter } from "./query";
import {
  toAdminCollab, toAdminCreator, toAdminPayment, toAdminPayout, toAnomaly, toFlaggedMessage, toOverview,
  toPayoutAccountFull, totalOf,
  type AdminCollab, type AdminCollabRow, type AdminCreator, type AdminCreatorRow, type AdminPayment, type AdminPaymentRow,
  type AdminPayout, type AdminPayoutRow, type Anomaly, type AnomalyRow, type FlaggedMessage, type FlaggedMessageRow,
  type Overview, type OverviewRow, type PayoutAccountFull, type PayoutAccountRow, type PayoutRecord,
} from "./view";

// 管理後台的伺服器端讀取。全部用管理員自己的 client（definer 函式用 admin_actor() 檢查、資料表靠 RLS 的 is_admin()）。
// 會讀 cookie：只能在 requireAdmin 之後呼叫。不快取。

export type Page<T> = { items: T[]; total: number };

export async function loadOverview(): Promise<Overview> {
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("admin_overview");
  if (error) throw error;
  return toOverview(((data ?? []) as unknown as OverviewRow[])[0]);
}

export async function loadCreators(status: CreatorFilter, q: string, page: number): Promise<Page<AdminCreator>> {
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("admin_list_creators", {
    p_status: status, p_query: q, p_limit: ADMIN_PAGE_SIZE, p_offset: offsetOf(page),
  });
  if (error) throw error;
  const rows = (data ?? []) as unknown as AdminCreatorRow[];
  return { items: rows.map(toAdminCreator), total: totalOf(rows) };
}

export async function loadFlaggedMessages(view: MessageFilter, page: number): Promise<Page<FlaggedMessage>> {
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("admin_list_flagged_messages", {
    p_view: view, p_limit: ADMIN_PAGE_SIZE, p_offset: offsetOf(page),
  });
  if (error) throw error;
  const rows = (data ?? []) as unknown as FlaggedMessageRow[];
  return { items: rows.map(toFlaggedMessage), total: totalOf(rows) };
}

export async function loadAdminCollabs(status: CollabFilter, q: string, page: number): Promise<Page<AdminCollab>> {
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("admin_list_collaborations", {
    p_status: status, p_query: q, p_limit: ADMIN_PAGE_SIZE, p_offset: offsetOf(page),
  });
  if (error) throw error;
  const rows = (data ?? []) as unknown as AdminCollabRow[];
  return { items: rows.map(toAdminCollab), total: totalOf(rows) };
}

type PaymentView = "all" | "pending" | "paid" | "refund";

/** 付款單列表；collabId 有值時只列這個合作案（合作案詳情用，最多 100 張）。 */
export async function loadAdminPayments(view: PaymentView, page: number, collabId: string | null = null): Promise<Page<AdminPayment>> {
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("admin_list_payments", {
    p_view: view,
    p_limit: collabId ? 100 : ADMIN_PAGE_SIZE,
    p_offset: collabId ? 0 : offsetOf(page),
    ...(collabId ? { p_collab_id: collabId } : {}),
  });
  if (error) throw error;
  const rows = (data ?? []) as unknown as AdminPaymentRow[];
  return { items: rows.map(toAdminPayment), total: totalOf(rows) };
}

export async function loadPayouts(page: number): Promise<Page<AdminPayout>> {
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("admin_list_payouts", { p_limit: ADMIN_PAGE_SIZE, p_offset: offsetOf(page) });
  if (error) throw error;
  const rows = (data ?? []) as unknown as AdminPayoutRow[];
  return { items: rows.map(toAdminPayout), total: totalOf(rows) };
}

const ANOMALY_COLUMNS =
  "id, created_at, source, kind, merchant_trade_no, provider_trade_no, expected_amount_twd, received_amount_twd, collaboration_id, resolved_at, resolved_note";

/** 付款異常（payment_anomalies 只有管理員讀得到）；open = 未處理，由舊到新；已處理由新到舊。 */
export async function loadAnomalies(open: boolean, page: number): Promise<Page<Anomaly>> {
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const base = supabase.from("payment_anomalies").select(ANOMALY_COLUMNS, { count: "exact" });
  const from = offsetOf(page);
  const { data, error, count } = await (open ? base.is("resolved_at", null) : base.not("resolved_at", "is", null))
    .order("created_at", { ascending: open })
    .order("id", { ascending: true })
    .range(from, from + ADMIN_PAGE_SIZE - 1);
  if (error) throw error;
  return { items: ((data ?? []) as unknown as AnomalyRow[]).map(toAnomaly), total: count ?? 0 };
}

/** 撥款畫面的完整收款帳戶（資料庫寫一筆 payout_account_viewed audit）；不是待撥款或沒有帳戶回傳 null。 */
export async function loadPayoutAccount(collabId: string): Promise<PayoutAccountFull | null> {
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("admin_get_payout_account", { p_collab_id: collabId });
  if (error) throw error;
  return toPayoutAccountFull(((data ?? []) as unknown as PayoutAccountRow[])[0]);
}

/** 已撥款的合作案：撥款紀錄（帳戶末四碼、轉帳說明）。 */
export async function loadPayoutRecord(collabId: string): Promise<PayoutRecord | null> {
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data, error } = await supabase.from("collaboration_payouts")
    .select("amount_twd, bank_code, account_last4, account_name, transfer_note, created_at")
    .eq("collaboration_id", collabId).maybeSingle();
  if (error) throw error;
  if (!data) return null;
  return {
    amount: data.amount_twd, bankCode: data.bank_code, accountLast4: data.account_last4,
    accountName: data.account_name, transferNote: data.transfer_note, createdAt: data.created_at,
  };
}
```

`src/app/admin/actions.ts`

```ts
"use server";

import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { requireAdmin } from "@/lib/auth/current-user";
import { createClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import { isUuid } from "@/lib/briefs/query";
import { adminErrorMessage, paymentResultNotice } from "@/lib/admin/errors";
import { cleanAdminNote, validateAdminNote } from "@/lib/admin/notes";
import { readEcpayConfig } from "@/lib/payments/config";
import { parseAmount } from "@/lib/validation/collab";

// 管理後台的寫入。每個 action：requireAdmin → 參數一律當 unknown 檢查 → 以管理員自己的 client 呼叫 definer 函式
// （資料庫用 admin_actor() 再檢查一次、寫 audit）。"use server" 檔案只能 export async 函式。

export type AdminActionResult = { ok: true; notice?: string } | { ok: false; error: string };

const BAD_INPUT = "資料格式不正確，請重新整理頁面";
const CREATOR_ACTIONS: readonly string[] = ["approve", "reject", "suspend", "unsuspend"];
const TRADE_NO_RE = /^KL[A-Za-z0-9]{1,18}$/;
const PROVIDER_TRADE_NO_RE = /^[A-Za-z0-9]{1,20}$/;

function revalidateAdmin() {
  revalidatePath("/admin", "layout");
}

function revalidateCollab(id: string) {
  revalidateAdmin();
  revalidatePath(`/brand/collabs/${id}`);
  revalidatePath(`/creator/collabs/${id}`);
  revalidatePath("/brand/collabs");
  revalidatePath("/creator/collabs");
}

function note(v: unknown, label: string): { ok: true; value: string } | { ok: false; error: string } {
  const n = cleanAdminNote(v);
  if (n === null) return { ok: false, error: BAD_INPUT };
  const err = validateAdminNote(n, label);
  return err ? { ok: false, error: err } : { ok: true, value: n };
}

/** 創作者審核：核准、退回（原因必填）、停權（原因必填）、解除停權。 */
export async function setCreatorStatusAction(userId: unknown, action: unknown, reason?: unknown): Promise<AdminActionResult> {
  await requireAdmin();
  if (!isUuid(userId) || typeof action !== "string" || !CREATOR_ACTIONS.includes(action)) return { ok: false, error: BAD_INPUT };
  let cleanReason: string | null = null;
  if (action === "reject" || action === "suspend") {
    const r = note(reason, "原因");
    if (!r.ok) return r;
    cleanReason = r.value;
  }
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { error } = await supabase.rpc("set_creator_status", {
    p_user_id: userId, p_action: action, ...(cleanReason !== null ? { p_reason: cleanReason } : {}),
  });
  if (error) return { ok: false, error: adminErrorMessage(error) };
  revalidateAdmin();
  // 公開頁與廣場不快取（followups「公開頁不快取」），創作者自己的頁面在下次請求就會看到新狀態
  revalidatePath("/creator", "layout");
  return { ok: true };
}

/** 違規訊息：標記已檢視（重複標記不報錯）。 */
export async function markMessageReviewedAction(messageId: unknown): Promise<AdminActionResult> {
  await requireAdmin();
  if (!isUuid(messageId)) return { ok: false, error: BAD_INPUT };
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { error } = await supabase.rpc("mark_message_reviewed", { p_message_id: messageId });
  if (error) return { ok: false, error: adminErrorMessage(error) };
  revalidateAdmin();
  return { ok: true };
}

/** 人工標記已付款：線下付款／超過線上付款上限（建 MAN 付款單 → record_payment_success）。 */
export async function recordOfflinePaymentAction(collabId: unknown, memo: unknown): Promise<AdminActionResult> {
  await requireAdmin();
  if (!isUuid(collabId)) return { ok: false, error: BAD_INPUT };
  const n = note(memo, "說明");
  if (!n.ok) return n;
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("admin_record_offline_payment", { p_collab_id: collabId, p_note: n.value });
  if (error) return { ok: false, error: adminErrorMessage(error) };
  revalidateCollab(collabId);
  const r = paymentResultNotice(data);
  return r.ok ? { ok: true, ...(r.notice ? { notice: r.notice } : {}) } : { ok: false, error: r.notice ?? "發生錯誤，請稍後再試" };
}

/**
 * 綠界已扣款、但沒收到通知（已在綠界廠商後台確認）：用既有的 KL 付款單 → record_payment_success。
 * 環境用這個部署的 ECPAY_ENV（KL 單是這個部署建立的）；綠界未設定時拒絕（請用 README 備用 SQL）。
 */
export async function recordLostPaymentAction(
  collabId: unknown, merchantTradeNo: unknown, providerTradeNo: unknown, amount: unknown, memo: unknown,
): Promise<AdminActionResult> {
  await requireAdmin();
  if (!isUuid(collabId) || typeof merchantTradeNo !== "string" || !TRADE_NO_RE.test(merchantTradeNo)
      || typeof providerTradeNo !== "string" || typeof amount !== "string") {
    return { ok: false, error: BAD_INPUT };
  }
  const ptn = providerTradeNo.trim();
  if (!PROVIDER_TRADE_NO_RE.test(ptn)) return { ok: false, error: "綠界交易編號是 1–20 位英數字" };
  const amountTwd = parseAmount(amount);
  if (amountTwd === null) return { ok: false, error: "請填綠界後台顯示的金額（整數）" };
  const n = note(memo, "說明");
  if (!n.ok) return n;
  const env = readEcpayConfig()?.env ?? null;
  if (!env) return { ok: false, error: "綠界環境未設定，無法確認這張付款單；請用 README 的備用 SQL" };
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("admin_record_lost_payment", {
    p_merchant_trade_no: merchantTradeNo, p_provider_trade_no: ptn, p_amount_twd: amountTwd, p_env: env, p_note: n.value,
  });
  if (error) return { ok: false, error: adminErrorMessage(error) };
  revalidateCollab(collabId);
  const r = paymentResultNotice(data);
  return r.ok ? { ok: true, ...(r.notice ? { notice: r.notice } : {}) } : { ok: false, error: r.notice ?? "發生錯誤，請稍後再試" };
}

/** 撥款：管理員已在平台外轉帳，標記 awaiting_payout → completed（轉帳說明必填）。 */
export async function markPayoutAction(collabId: unknown, memo: unknown): Promise<AdminActionResult> {
  await requireAdmin();
  if (!isUuid(collabId)) return { ok: false, error: BAD_INPUT };
  const n = note(memo, "轉帳說明");
  if (!n.ok) return n;
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { error } = await supabase.rpc("admin_mark_payout", { p_collab_id: collabId, p_note: n.value });
  if (error) return { ok: false, error: adminErrorMessage(error) };
  revalidateCollab(collabId);
  return { ok: true };
}

/** 平台取消（爭議處理）：原因必填、雙方看得到；已付款的款項自動標記需退款。 */
export async function adminCancelAction(collabId: unknown, reason: unknown): Promise<AdminActionResult> {
  await requireAdmin();
  if (!isUuid(collabId)) return { ok: false, error: BAD_INPUT };
  const n = note(reason, "原因");
  if (!n.ok) return n;
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("admin_cancel_collaboration", { p_collab_id: collabId, p_reason: n.value });
  if (error) return { ok: false, error: adminErrorMessage(error) };
  revalidateCollab(collabId);
  const flagged = typeof data === "number" ? data : 0;
  return flagged > 0
    ? { ok: true, notice: `已取消。${flagged} 筆已付款的款項標記為需退款（款項 → 需退款）` }
    : { ok: true };
}

/** 退款完成（實際退款在綠界後台或另外轉帳）：清除需退款、記錄說明。 */
export async function resolveRefundAction(paymentId: unknown, memo: unknown): Promise<AdminActionResult> {
  await requireAdmin();
  if (!isUuid(paymentId)) return { ok: false, error: BAD_INPUT };
  const n = note(memo, "退款說明");
  if (!n.ok) return n;
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { error } = await supabase.rpc("resolve_payment_refund", { p_payment_id: paymentId, p_note: n.value });
  if (error) return { ok: false, error: adminErrorMessage(error) };
  revalidateAdmin();
  return { ok: true };
}

/** 付款異常處理完成：記錄說明。 */
export async function resolveAnomalyAction(anomalyId: unknown, memo: unknown): Promise<AdminActionResult> {
  await requireAdmin();
  if (typeof anomalyId !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(anomalyId) || anomalyId <= 0) return { ok: false, error: BAD_INPUT };
  const n = note(memo, "處理說明");
  if (!n.ok) return n;
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { error } = await supabase.rpc("resolve_payment_anomaly", { p_id: anomalyId, p_note: n.value });
  if (error) return { ok: false, error: adminErrorMessage(error) };
  revalidateAdmin();
  return { ok: true };
}
```

`src/app/creator/me/payout/actions.ts`

```ts
"use server";

import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { requireRole } from "@/lib/auth/current-user";
import { createClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import { parsePayoutAccountForm, validatePayoutAccount } from "@/lib/payout-account";

export type PayoutFormState = { ok?: boolean; fieldErrors?: Record<string, string>; formError?: string };

const FIELD_BY_DB: Record<string, string> = {
  "invalid bank_code": "bankCode",
  "invalid branch_code": "branchCode",
  "invalid account_number": "accountNumber",
  "invalid account_name": "accountName",
};

/** 創作者設定收款帳戶（set_my_payout_account；完整帳號不會再回傳給瀏覽器）。 */
export async function savePayoutAccountAction(_prev: PayoutFormState, fd: FormData): Promise<PayoutFormState> {
  await requireRole("creator");
  const input = parsePayoutAccountForm(fd);
  const fieldErrors = validatePayoutAccount(input);
  if (fieldErrors) return { fieldErrors };
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { error } = await supabase.rpc("set_my_payout_account", {
    p_bank_code: input.bankCode,
    p_branch_code: input.branchCode,
    p_account_number: input.accountNumber,
    p_account_name: input.accountName,
  });
  if (error) {
    const m = error.message ?? "";
    if (Object.hasOwn(FIELD_BY_DB, m)) return { fieldErrors: { [FIELD_BY_DB[m]]: "格式不正確" } };
    return { formError: "發生錯誤，請稍後再試" };
  }
  revalidatePath("/creator/me/payout");
  return { ok: true };
}
```

`src/components/admin/FilterChips.tsx`

```tsx
import Link from "next/link";

/** 篩選標籤（連結，寫進網址）。選到的那個 aria-current="page"。 */
export function FilterChips<T extends string>({ label, options, current, hrefFor }: {
  label: string; options: { value: T; label: string }[]; current: T; hrefFor: (v: T) => string;
}) {
  return (
    <nav aria-label={label} className="-mx-4 overflow-x-auto px-4 md:mx-0 md:px-0">
      <ul className="flex w-max gap-2">
        {options.map((o) => {
          const active = o.value === current;
          return (
            <li key={o.value}>
              <Link
                href={hrefFor(o.value)} aria-current={active ? "page" : undefined}
                className={`inline-flex h-11 items-center rounded-full border px-4 text-sm font-semibold focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ink ${active ? "border-ink bg-ink text-on-ink" : "border-line bg-surface text-ink"}`}
              >
                {o.label}
              </Link>
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </ul>
    </nav>
  );
}
```

`src/components/admin/Pagination.tsx`

```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { pageCount } from "@/lib/admin/query";

/** 上一頁／下一頁（頁數在網址 ?page=）。只有一頁時不顯示。 */
export function Pagination({ page, total, hrefFor }: { page: number; total: number; hrefFor: (page: number) => string }) {
  const pages = pageCount(total);
  if (pages <= 1 && page <= 1) return null;
  const btn = "inline-flex h-11 items-center rounded-btn border border-line bg-surface px-4 text-sm font-semibold text-ink";
  return (
    <nav aria-label="分頁" className="mt-4 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
      {page > 1 ? <Link href={hrefFor(Math.min(page - 1, pages))} className={btn}>上一頁</Link> : <span />}
      <span className="text-sm text-muted">第 {Math.min(page, pages)} / {pages} 頁，共 {total} 筆</span>
      {page < pages ? <Link href={hrefFor(page + 1)} className={btn}>下一頁</Link> : <span />}
    </nav>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { inputClassName } from "./styles";

/** 關鍵字搜尋（GET 表單；送出後回到第 1 頁，其他篩選放在 hidden 欄位）。 */
export function SearchForm({ action, q, placeholder, hidden }: {
  action: string; q: string; placeholder: string; hidden: Record<string, string>;
}) {
  return (
    <form action={action} method="get" role="search" className="flex w-full max-w-md gap-2">
      {Object.entries(hidden).map(([k, v]) => <input key={k} type="hidden" name={k} value={v} />)}
      <label className="sr-only" htmlFor="admin-q">搜尋</label>
      <input id="admin-q" name="q" defaultValue={q} maxLength={50} placeholder={placeholder} className={inputClassName} />
      <button type="submit" className="h-[42px] shrink-0 rounded-btn bg-ink px-4 text-sm font-semibold text-on-ink">搜尋</button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

`src/components/admin/NoteAction.tsx`

```tsx
"use client";

import { useId, useState, useTransition } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { isStaleStateMessage } from "@/lib/briefs/errors";
import { ADMIN_NOTE_MAX, validateAdminNote } from "@/lib/admin/notes";
import { textLength } from "@/lib/validation/brief";

type Result = { ok: true; notice?: string } | { ok: false; error: string };

type Props = {
  label: string;
  /** 展開後的說明（例如「已付款的款項會標記為需退款」） */
  message?: string;
  /** 輸入框的標籤，例如「退回原因（創作者看得到）」 */
  fieldLabel: string;
  /** validateAdminNote 的欄位名稱（錯誤訊息用） */
  noteName: string;
  confirmLabel: string;
  danger?: boolean;
  onSubmit: (note: string) => Promise<Result>;
};

/** 管理員動作：頁面內展開的確認區塊，原因／說明必填、最多 500 字（不用瀏覽器的 confirm()）。 */
export function NoteAction({ label, message, fieldLabel, noteName, confirmLabel, danger, onSubmit }: Props) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [note, setNote] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [pending, startTransition] = useTransition();
  const id = useId();
  const count = textLength(note);

  function submit() {
    const err = validateAdminNote(note.trim(), noteName);
    if (err) return setError(err);
    setError(null);
    startTransition(async () => {
      try {
        const res = await onSubmit(note);
        if (res.ok) {
          setOpen(false);
          setNote("");
          setNotice(res.notice ?? null);
        } else if (isStaleStateMessage(res.error)) {
          setOpen(false);
          setNotice("狀態已更新，已為你重新整理");
          router.refresh();
        } else {
          setError(res.error);
        }
      } catch {
        setError("發生錯誤，請稍後再試");
      }
    });
  }

  const tone = danger ? "border-danger text-danger" : "border-line text-ink";
  if (!open) {
    return (
      <div className="w-full sm:w-auto">
        <button
          type="button" onClick={() => { setNotice(null); setOpen(true); }} aria-expanded={false}
          className={`h-10 w-full rounded-btn border bg-surface px-4 text-sm font-semibold sm:w-auto ${tone}`}
        >
          {label}
        </button>
        {notice && <p role="status" className="mt-2 break-words text-sm text-muted">{notice}</p>}
      </div>
    );
  }
  return (
    <div role="group" aria-label={label} className="w-full rounded-card border border-line bg-surface p-4">
      {message && <p className="mb-2 text-sm text-ink">{message}</p>}
      <label htmlFor={id} className="text-sm font-semibold text-ink">{fieldLabel}</label>
      <textarea
        id={id} rows={3} value={note} onChange={(e) => setNote(e.target.value)} disabled={pending}
        className="mt-1 w-full rounded-input border border-line bg-surface px-3 py-2 outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-ink"
      />
      <p aria-live="polite" className={`text-right text-xs ${count > ADMIN_NOTE_MAX ? "font-semibold text-danger" : "text-muted"}`}>
        {count}/{ADMIN_NOTE_MAX}
      </p>
      <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        <button
          type="button" onClick={submit} disabled={pending}
          className={`h-10 rounded-btn px-4 text-sm font-semibold text-on-ink disabled:opacity-60 ${danger ? "bg-danger" : "bg-ink"}`}
        >
          {pending ? "處理中…" : confirmLabel}
        </button>
        <button
          type="button" onClick={() => { setOpen(false); setError(null); }} disabled={pending}
          className="h-10 rounded-btn border border-line px-4 text-sm font-semibold text-ink disabled:opacity-60"
        >
          取消
        </button>
      </div>
      {error && <p role="alert" className="mt-2 text-sm text-danger">{error}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { Suspense } from "react";
import { requireAdmin } from "@/lib/auth/current-user";
import { AdminSideFooter } from "@/components/admin/AdminSideFooter";
import { BottomTabBar } from "@/components/shell/BottomTabBar";
import { ADMIN_NAV } from "@/components/shell/nav";
import { SideNav } from "@/components/shell/SideNav";
import { SignOutButton } from "@/components/shell/SignOutButton";

// 與品牌、創作者區相同的殼：桌面左側 SideNav、手機底部導覽列（5 項）。
export default function AdminLayout({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen bg-bg">
      <aside className="hidden shrink-0 md:block">
        <Suspense fallback={<div aria-hidden className="h-screen w-24" />}><SideNav items={ADMIN_NAV} footer={<AdminSideFooter />} /></Suspense>
      </aside>
      <main className="min-w-0 flex-1 bg-panel px-4 pb-[calc(6rem+env(safe-area-inset-bottom))] pt-6 md:my-3 md:mr-3 md:rounded-card md:py-8 md:pl-8 md:pr-7 md:pb-8">
        <Suspense fallback={<p className="text-muted">載入中…</p>}>
          <Gate>{children}</Gate>
        </Suspense>
      </main>
      <Suspense fallback={null}><BottomTabBar items={ADMIN_NAV} /></Suspense>
    </div>
  );
}

async function Gate({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  await requireAdmin();
  return (
    <>
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between md:hidden">
        <p className="font-semibold">管理後台</p>
        <SignOutButton />
      </div>
      {children}
    </>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { Suspense } from "react";
import { requireAdmin } from "@/lib/auth/current-user";
import { loadOverview } from "@/lib/admin/load";

export default function AdminHomePage() {
  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="text-[32px] font-bold text-ink">管理後台</h1>
      <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted">待處理的事項。數字是目前的筆數，點進去處理。</p>
      <Suspense fallback={<div aria-busy="true" className="mt-6 h-40 rounded-card border border-line bg-surface" />}>
        <Cards />
      </Suspense>
    </div>
  );
}

async function Cards() {
  await requireAdmin();
  const o = await loadOverview();
  const cards = [
    { label: "待審核創作者", n: o.pendingCreators, href: "/admin/creators" },
    { label: "待撥款", n: o.awaitingPayout, href: "/admin/payments" },
    { label: "需退款", n: o.refundRequired, href: "/admin/payments?view=refunds" },
    { label: "付款異常", n: o.openAnomalies, href: "/admin/payments?view=anomalies" },
    { label: "未檢視的違規訊息", n: o.openFlaggedMessages, href: "/admin/messages" },
    { label: "等待付款的合作案", n: o.awaitingPayment, href: "/admin/collabs?status=awaiting_payment" },
  ];
  return (
    <ul className="mt-6 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 md:grid-cols-3">
      {cards.map((c) => (
        <li key={c.label}>
          <Link
            href={c.href}
            className="block rounded-card border border-line bg-surface p-4 hover:bg-primary-soft focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ink md:p-5"
          >
            <p className="text-sm text-muted">{c.label}</p>
            <p className={`mt-1 text-3xl font-bold ${c.n > 0 ? "text-ink" : "text-muted"}`}>{c.n}</p>
          </Link>
        </li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  );
}
```

`src/components/admin/CreatorReviewActions.tsx`

```tsx
"use client";

import { setCreatorStatusAction } from "@/app/admin/actions";
import { ConfirmAction } from "@/components/briefs/ConfirmAction";
import type { CreatorReviewAction } from "@/lib/admin/view";
import { NoteAction } from "./NoteAction";

/** 一位創作者的審核按鈕：核准／解除停權用確認區塊，退回／停權要寫原因（創作者看得到）。 */
export function CreatorReviewActions({ userId, handle, actions }: { userId: string; handle: string; actions: CreatorReviewAction[] }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
      {actions.includes("approve") && (
        <ConfirmAction
          label="核准" confirmLabel="確定核准"
          message={`核准 @${handle}：公開頁立刻上線、可以報名招募與接案，網址代稱之後不能再改。`}
          onConfirm={() => setCreatorStatusAction(userId, "approve")}
        />
      )}
      {actions.includes("reject") && (
        <NoteAction
          label="退回" fieldLabel="退回原因（創作者看得到）" noteName="原因" confirmLabel="確定退回" danger
          message="創作者會在頁面上方看到原因，修正後可以重新送審。"
          onSubmit={(reason) => setCreatorStatusAction(userId, "reject", reason)}
        />
      )}
      {actions.includes("suspend") && (
        <NoteAction
          label="停權" fieldLabel="停權原因（創作者看得到）" noteName="原因" confirmLabel="確定停權" danger
          message="停權後公開頁下架、所有對話暫停（雙方都不能再傳訊息），進行中的合作案不受影響。"
          onSubmit={(reason) => setCreatorStatusAction(userId, "suspend", reason)}
        />
      )}
      {actions.includes("unsuspend") && (
        <ConfirmAction
          label="解除停權" confirmLabel="確定解除"
          message={`解除 @${handle} 的停權：公開頁重新上線、對話恢復。`}
          onConfirm={() => setCreatorStatusAction(userId, "unsuspend")}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`src/components/admin/CreatorReviewList.tsx`

```tsx
import { formatDateTimeTw } from "@/lib/briefs/dates";
import { formatNumber } from "@/lib/format";
import { CREATOR_STATUS_LABEL, creatorActions, igUrl, type AdminCreator } from "@/lib/admin/view";
import { CreatorReviewActions } from "./CreatorReviewActions";
import { emptyClassName } from "./styles";

/** 審核列表：一列一位創作者（手機也是直式卡片，不用表格）。 */
export function CreatorReviewList({ items }: { items: AdminCreator[] }) {
  if (items.length === 0) return <p className={emptyClassName}>沒有符合的創作者</p>;
  return (
    <ul className="mt-4 divide-y divide-line overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-surface">
      {items.map((c) => (
        <li key={c.userId} className="space-y-2 p-4 md:p-5">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            <p className="min-w-0 break-words font-semibold text-ink">{c.displayName}</p>
            <span className="rounded-full bg-primary-soft px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold text-ink">{CREATOR_STATUS_LABEL[c.status]}</span>
          </div>
          <dl className="grid grid-cols-[auto_1fr] gap-x-3 gap-y-1 text-sm">
            <dt className="text-muted">網址代稱</dt><dd className="min-w-0 break-all">@{c.handle}</dd>
            <dt className="text-muted">IG</dt>
            <dd className="min-w-0 break-all">
              <a href={igUrl(c.igUsername)} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="text-primary underline">
                {c.igUsername}
              </a>
              <span className="text-muted">（自填粉絲 {formatNumber(c.followerCount)}）</span>
            </dd>
            {c.verificationCode && (
              <>
                <dt className="text-muted">驗證碼</dt>
                <dd><b className="rounded-btn bg-ink px-2 py-0.5 tracking-widest text-on-ink">{c.verificationCode}</b></dd>
              </>
            )}
            <dt className="text-muted">地區／領域</dt>
            <dd className="min-w-0 break-words">{[c.region, ...c.categories].filter(Boolean).join("、") || "—"}</dd>
            <dt className="text-muted">申請時間</dt><dd>{c.createdAt ? formatDateTimeTw(c.createdAt) : "—"}</dd>
            {c.reviewNote && (
              <>
                <dt className="text-muted">原因</dt><dd className="min-w-0 whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">{c.reviewNote}</dd>
              </>
            )}
          </dl>
          <CreatorReviewActions userId={c.userId} handle={c.handle} actions={creatorActions(c.status)} />
        </li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  );
}
```

`src/app/admin/creators/page.tsx`

```tsx
import { Suspense } from "react";
import { requireAdmin } from "@/lib/auth/current-user";
import { loadCreators } from "@/lib/admin/load";
import { adminHref, CREATOR_FILTERS, parseCreatorFilter, parsePage, parseQuery, type SearchParams } from "@/lib/admin/query";
import { CreatorReviewList } from "@/components/admin/CreatorReviewList";
import { FilterChips } from "@/components/admin/FilterChips";
import { Pagination } from "@/components/admin/Pagination";
import { SearchForm } from "@/components/admin/SearchForm";

type Props = { searchParams: Promise<SearchParams> };

export default function AdminCreatorsPage({ searchParams }: Props) {
  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="text-[32px] font-bold text-ink">創作者審核</h1>
      <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted">打開 IG，確認自我介紹裡有驗證碼、粉絲數合理，再核准。</p>
      <Suspense fallback={<div aria-busy="true" className="mt-6 h-48 rounded-card border border-line bg-surface" />}>
        <Section searchParams={searchParams} />
      </Suspense>
    </div>
  );
}

async function Section({ searchParams }: Props) {
  await requireAdmin();
  const sp = await searchParams;
  const status = parseCreatorFilter(sp.status);
  const q = parseQuery(sp.q);
  const page = parsePage(sp.page);
  const { items, total } = await loadCreators(status, q, page);
  const href = (p: { status?: string; page?: number }) =>
    adminHref("/admin/creators", { status: p.status ?? status, q, page: p.page ?? 1 }, { status: "pending" });
  return (
    <div className="mt-6 space-y-4">
      <FilterChips label="審核狀態" options={CREATOR_FILTERS} current={status} hrefFor={(v) => href({ status: v })} />
      <SearchForm action="/admin/creators" q={q} placeholder="網址代稱、IG 或名稱" hidden={status === "pending" ? {} : { status }} />
      <CreatorReviewList items={items} />
      <Pagination page={page} total={total} hrefFor={(p) => href({ page: p })} />
    </div>
  );
}
```

`src/components/admin/ReviewButton.tsx`

```tsx
"use client";

import { useState, useTransition } from "react";
import { markMessageReviewedAction } from "@/app/admin/actions";

/** 標記已檢視：不會改變任何人的資料，所以不需要確認區塊；進行中 disabled。 */
export function ReviewButton({ messageId }: { messageId: string }) {
  const [pending, startTransition] = useTransition();
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  return (
    <div>
      <button
        type="button" disabled={pending}
        onClick={() => {
          setError(null);
          startTransition(async () => {
            try {
              const res = await markMessageReviewedAction(messageId);
              if (!res.ok) setError(res.error);
            } catch {
              setError("發生錯誤，請稍後再試");
            }
          });
        }}
        className="h-10 rounded-btn border border-line bg-surface px-4 text-sm font-semibold text-ink disabled:opacity-60"
      >
        {pending ? "處理中…" : "標記已檢視"}
      </button>
      {error && <p role="alert" className="mt-2 text-sm text-danger">{error}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { Suspense } from "react";
import { requireAdmin } from "@/lib/auth/current-user";
import { loadFlaggedMessages } from "@/lib/admin/load";
import { adminHref, MESSAGE_FILTERS, parseMessageFilter, parsePage, type SearchParams } from "@/lib/admin/query";
import { formatDateTimeTw } from "@/lib/briefs/dates";
import { FilterChips } from "@/components/admin/FilterChips";
import { Pagination } from "@/components/admin/Pagination";
import { ReviewButton } from "@/components/admin/ReviewButton";
import { emptyClassName } from "@/components/admin/styles";

type Props = { searchParams: Promise<SearchParams> };

export default function AdminMessagesPage({ searchParams }: Props) {
  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="text-[32px] font-bold text-ink">違規訊息</h1>
      <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted">
        含電話、Email、LINE 或外部連結的訊息（自動標記，可能誤判）。要處理的話到「創作者審核」停權，或到合作案平台取消。
      </p>
      <Suspense fallback={<div aria-busy="true" className="mt-6 h-48 rounded-card border border-line bg-surface" />}>
        <Section searchParams={searchParams} />
      </Suspense>
    </div>
  );
}

async function Section({ searchParams }: Props) {
  await requireAdmin();
  const sp = await searchParams;
  const view = parseMessageFilter(sp.view);
  const page = parsePage(sp.page);
  const { items, total } = await loadFlaggedMessages(view, page);
  const href = (p: { view?: string; page?: number }) =>
    adminHref("/admin/messages", { view: p.view ?? view, page: p.page ?? 1 }, { view: "open" });
  return (
    <div className="mt-6 space-y-4">
      <FilterChips label="檢視狀態" options={MESSAGE_FILTERS} current={view} hrefFor={(v) => href({ view: v })} />
      {items.length === 0 ? (
        <p className={emptyClassName}>{view === "open" ? "沒有待檢視的訊息" : "沒有訊息"}</p>
      ) : (
        <ul className="divide-y divide-line overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-surface">
          {items.map((m) => (
            <li key={m.id} className="space-y-2 p-4 md:p-5">
              <p className="text-xs text-muted">
                {m.createdAt ? formatDateTimeTw(m.createdAt) : ""}・{m.senderSide === "brand" ? "品牌" : "創作者"}傳送
              </p>
              <p className="text-sm">
                <span className="font-semibold">{m.brandName}</span>
                <span className="text-muted"> ↔ </span>
                {m.creatorHandle ? (
                  <Link href={adminHref("/admin/creators", { status: "all", q: m.creatorHandle })} className="font-semibold text-primary underline">
                    @{m.creatorHandle}
                  </Link>
                ) : <span className="font-semibold">{m.creatorName}</span>}
              </p>
              <p className="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words rounded-btn bg-primary-soft p-3 text-sm text-ink">{m.body}</p>
              {m.reviewedAt ? (
                <p className="text-xs text-muted">已檢視：{formatDateTimeTw(m.reviewedAt)}</p>
              ) : (
                <ReviewButton messageId={m.id} />
              )}
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
      <Pagination page={page} total={total} hrefFor={(p) => href({ page: p })} />
    </div>
  );
}
```

`src/components/admin/RefundAction.tsx`

```tsx
"use client";

import { resolveAnomalyAction, resolveRefundAction } from "@/app/admin/actions";
import { NoteAction } from "./NoteAction";

/** 需退款的款項：在綠界後台退款（或 ATM 另外轉帳）後，記錄退款說明。 */
export function RefundAction({ paymentId }: { paymentId: string }) {
  return (
    <NoteAction
      label="已退款" fieldLabel="退款說明（方式與日期）" noteName="退款說明" confirmLabel="記錄已退款"
      message="先在綠界廠商後台用付款單號辦理退款（信用卡刷退；ATM 要向品牌取得帳號另外轉帳），完成後再記錄。"
      onSubmit={(memo) => resolveRefundAction(paymentId, memo)}
    />
  );
}

/** 付款異常：在綠界後台確認並處理（退款或人工標記已付款）後，記錄處理說明。 */
export function AnomalyAction({ anomalyId }: { anomalyId: number }) {
  return (
    <NoteAction
      label="已處理" fieldLabel="處理說明" noteName="處理說明" confirmLabel="記錄已處理"
      onSubmit={(memo) => resolveAnomalyAction(anomalyId, memo)}
    />
  );
}
```

```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { formatDateTimeTw } from "@/lib/briefs/dates";
import { caseCode, COLLAB_STATUS_LABEL } from "@/lib/collabs/view";
import { formatTwd } from "@/lib/format";
import { PAYMENT_STATUS_LABEL, paymentSourceLabel, type AdminPayment } from "@/lib/admin/view";
import { RefundAction } from "./RefundAction";
import { emptyClassName } from "./styles";

/** 付款單列表（合作案詳情與「款項」頁共用）。withCollab：顯示合作案連結（款項頁）。 */
export function PaymentRows({ items, withCollab, empty }: { items: AdminPayment[]; withCollab: boolean; empty: string }) {
  if (items.length === 0) return <p className={emptyClassName}>{empty}</p>;
  return (
    <ul className="divide-y divide-line overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-surface">
      {items.map((p) => (
        <li key={p.id} className="space-y-2 p-4 md:p-5">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            <span className="min-w-0 break-all font-mono text-sm font-semibold text-ink">{p.merchantTradeNo}</span>
            <span className="rounded-full bg-primary-soft px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold text-ink">{PAYMENT_STATUS_LABEL[p.status]}</span>
            {p.env === "stage" && p.status === "paid" && (
              <span className="rounded-full border border-danger px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold text-danger">測試付款（未實際收款）</span>
            )}
            {p.refundRequired && (
              <span className="rounded-full bg-ink px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold text-on-ink">需退款</span>
            )}
            {p.refundedAt && <span className="rounded-full bg-success-soft px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold text-success">已退款</span>}
          </div>
          <dl className="grid grid-cols-[auto_1fr] gap-x-3 gap-y-1 text-sm">
            {withCollab && (
              <>
                <dt className="text-muted">合作案</dt>
                <dd className="min-w-0 break-words">
                  <Link href={`/admin/collabs/${p.collabId}`} className="text-primary underline">
                    {caseCode(p.collabId)} {p.collabTitle}
                  </Link>
                  <span className="text-muted">（{COLLAB_STATUS_LABEL[p.collabStatus]}・{p.brandName} × @{p.creatorHandle}）</span>
                </dd>
              </>
            )}
            <dt className="text-muted">金額／方式</dt><dd>{formatTwd(p.amount)}・{paymentSourceLabel(p)}</dd>
            <dt className="text-muted">建立</dt><dd>{p.createdAt ? formatDateTimeTw(p.createdAt) : "—"}</dd>
            {p.paidAt && (<><dt className="text-muted">付款</dt><dd>{formatDateTimeTw(p.paidAt)}</dd></>)}
            {p.providerTradeNo && (<><dt className="text-muted">綠界交易編號</dt><dd className="min-w-0 break-all font-mono">{p.providerTradeNo}</dd></>)}
            {p.adminNote && (<><dt className="text-muted">人工說明</dt><dd className="min-w-0 whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">{p.adminNote}</dd></>)}
            {p.refundNote && (<><dt className="text-muted">退款說明</dt><dd className="min-w-0 whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">{p.refundNote}</dd></>)}
          </dl>
          {p.refundRequired && <RefundAction paymentId={p.id} />}
        </li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { formatDateTw } from "@/lib/briefs/dates";
import { caseCode } from "@/lib/collabs/view";
import { formatTwd } from "@/lib/format";
import { maskAccount } from "@/lib/payout-account";
import { payoutBlocker, type AdminPayout } from "@/lib/admin/view";
import { emptyClassName } from "./styles";

/** 待撥款列表：帳戶只顯示末四碼；完整帳號在合作案詳情的撥款區（會記錄查看）。 */
export function PayoutList({ items }: { items: AdminPayout[] }) {
  if (items.length === 0) return <p className={emptyClassName}>沒有待撥款的合作案</p>;
  return (
    <ul className="divide-y divide-line overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-surface">
      {items.map((p) => {
        const blocker = payoutBlocker({ testPaid: p.testPaid, refundPending: p.refundPending, hasAccount: p.bankCode !== null });
        return (
          <li key={p.id}>
            <Link
              href={`/admin/collabs/${p.id}`}
              className="block space-y-1 p-4 hover:bg-primary-soft focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-inset focus-visible:ring-ink md:p-5"
            >
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                <span className="font-mono text-xs text-muted">{caseCode(p.id)}</span>
                <p className="min-w-0 break-words font-semibold text-ink">{p.title}</p>
                <span className="font-semibold text-primary">{formatTwd(p.amount)}</span>
              </div>
              <p className="min-w-0 break-words text-sm text-muted">
                @{p.creatorHandle}（{p.creatorName}）・{p.brandName}・{p.waitingSince ? `${formatDateTw(p.waitingSince)} 起等待` : ""}
              </p>
              <p className="text-sm">
                {p.bankCode && p.accountLast4 ? `收款帳戶 ${p.bankCode} ${maskAccount(p.accountLast4)}` : "沒有收款帳戶"}
              </p>
              {blocker && <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-danger">{blocker}</p>}
            </Link>
          </li>
        );
      })}
    </ul>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { formatDateTimeTw } from "@/lib/briefs/dates";
import { caseCode } from "@/lib/collabs/view";
import { formatTwd } from "@/lib/format";
import type { Anomaly } from "@/lib/admin/view";
import { AnomalyAction } from "./RefundAction";
import { emptyClassName } from "./styles";

export function AnomalyList({ items, open }: { items: Anomaly[]; open: boolean }) {
  if (items.length === 0) return <p className={emptyClassName}>{open ? "沒有未處理的付款異常" : "沒有已處理的付款異常"}</p>;
  return (
    <ul className="divide-y divide-line overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-surface">
      {items.map((a) => (
        <li key={a.id} className="space-y-2 p-4 md:p-5">
          <p className="font-semibold text-ink">{a.kind}<span className="ml-2 text-xs font-normal text-muted">{a.source}・{formatDateTimeTw(a.createdAt)}</span></p>
          <dl className="grid grid-cols-[auto_1fr] gap-x-3 gap-y-1 text-sm">
            <dt className="text-muted">綠界訂單編號</dt><dd className="min-w-0 break-all font-mono">{a.merchantTradeNo || "—"}</dd>
            <dt className="text-muted">綠界交易編號</dt><dd className="min-w-0 break-all font-mono">{a.providerTradeNo || "—"}</dd>
            <dt className="text-muted">應付／實收</dt>
            <dd>{a.expected !== null ? formatTwd(a.expected) : "—"} ／ {a.received !== null ? formatTwd(a.received) : "—"}</dd>
            {a.collabId && (
              <>
                <dt className="text-muted">合作案</dt>
                <dd><Link href={`/admin/collabs/${a.collabId}`} className="text-primary underline">{caseCode(a.collabId)}</Link></dd>
              </>
            )}
            {a.resolvedNote && (<><dt className="text-muted">處理說明</dt><dd className="min-w-0 whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">{a.resolvedNote}</dd></>)}
          </dl>
          {open && <AnomalyAction anomalyId={a.id} />}
        </li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  );
}
```
